                    scale: Vec3::from_array(scale),
                },
                None,
            ),
            gltf::scene::Transform::Matrix { matrix } => (
                NodeTrs {
                    translation: Vec3::ZERO,
                    rotation: glam::Quat::IDENTITY,
                    scale: Vec3::ONE,
                },
                Some(Mat4::from_cols_array_2d(&matrix)),
            ),
        };
        if !trs.translation.is_finite()
            || !trs.rotation.is_finite()
            || !trs.scale.is_finite()
            || (trs.rotation.length_squared() - 1.).abs() > 0.001
            || matrix.is_some_and(|m| {
                !m.is_finite()
                    || m.x_axis.w != 0.
                    || m.y_axis.w != 0.
                    || m.z_axis.w != 0.
                    || m.w_axis.w != 1.
            })
        {
            return Err("Invalid node transform".into());
        }
        let weights = node.weights().map(|w| w.to_vec());
        if let Some(w) = &weights {
            if node
                .mesh()
                .is_none_or(|m| w.len() != asset.meshes[m.index()].weights.len())
                || w.iter().any(|v| !v.is_finite())
            {
                return Err("Invalid node morph weights".into());
            }
        }
        asset.nodes.push(ModelNode {
            name: node.name().map(str::to_owned),
            children: node.children().map(|n| n.index()).collect(),
            mesh: node.mesh().map(|m| m.index()),
            skin: node.skin().map(|s| s.index()),
            camera: node.camera().map(|c| c.index()),
            trs,
            matrix,
            weights,
        });
    }
    for skin in gltf.skins() {
        let joints: Vec<_> = skin.joints().map(|j| j.index()).collect();
        if joints.is_empty() || joints.len() > 65536 {
            return Err("Invalid skin joint count".into());
        }
        if let Some(ibm) = skin.inverse_bind_matrices() {
            checked_attribute(&ibm, joints.len(), A::Mat4, &[D::F32], false)?;
        }
        let inverse_bind_matrices: Vec<_> = skin
            .reader(|b| buffers.get(b.index()).copied())
            .read_inverse_bind_matrices()
            .map(|m| m.map(|v| Mat4::from_cols_array_2d(&v)).collect())
            .unwrap_or_else(|| vec![Mat4::IDENTITY; joints.len()]);
        if inverse_bind_matrices.iter().any(|m| !m.is_finite()) {
            return Err("Invalid inverse bind matrices".into());
        }
        asset.skins.push(ModelSkin {
            name: skin.name().map(str::to_owned),
            joints,
            inverse_bind_matrices,
            skeleton: skin.skeleton().map(|n| n.index()),
        });
    }
    for node in &asset.nodes {
        if let (Some(mesh), Some(skin)) = (node.mesh, node.skin) {
            for &p in &asset.meshes[mesh].primitives {
                for v in &asset.vertices[asset.primitives[p].vertices.clone()] {
                    if v.influences.is_empty()
                        || v.influences
                            .iter()
                            .any(|&(joint, _)| joint as usize >= asset.skins[skin].joints.len())
                    {
                        return Err(
                            "Skinned primitive has missing or invalid joint influences".into()
                        );
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    let mut expanded_animation_scalars = 0usize;
    for animation in gltf.animations() {
        let mut clip = AnimationClip {
            name: animation.name().map(str::to_owned),
            channels: vec![],
            duration: 0.,
        };
        let mut targets = std::collections::HashSet::new();
        for channel in animation.channels() {
            let node = channel.target().node().index();
            let property = match channel.target().property() {
                gltf::animation::Property::Translation => AnimationProperty::Translation,
                gltf::animation::Property::Rotation => AnimationProperty::Rotation,
                gltf::animation::Property::Scale => AnimationProperty::Scale,
                gltf::animation::Property::MorphTargetWeights => AnimationProperty::Weights,
            };
            if !targets.insert((node, property as u8)) {
                return Err("Animation channels duplicate a node property".into());
            }
            if property != AnimationProperty::Weights && asset.nodes[node].matrix.is_some() {
                return Err("Matrix-authored nodes cannot have TRS animations".into());
            }
            let components = match property {
                AnimationProperty::Rotation => 4,
                AnimationProperty::Weights => asset.nodes[node]
                    .mesh
                    .map(|m| asset.meshes[m].weights.len())
                    .ok_or("Weight animation targets node without mesh")?,
                _ => 3,
            };
            let sampler = channel.sampler();
            let interpolation = match sampler.interpolation() {
                gltf::animation::Interpolation::Step => Interpolation::Step,
                gltf::animation::Interpolation::Linear => Interpolation::Linear,
                gltf::animation::Interpolation::CubicSpline => Interpolation::CubicSpline,
            };
            let input = sampler.input();
            checked_attribute(&input, input.count(), A::Scalar, &[D::F32], false)?;
            let output = sampler.output();
            checked_attribute(
                &output,
                output.count(),
                match property {
                    AnimationProperty::Rotation => A::Vec4,
                    AnimationProperty::Weights => A::Scalar,
                    _ => A::Vec3,
                },
                &[D::F32],
                false,
            )?;
            let output_scalars = output
                .count()
                .checked_mul(output.dimensions().multiplicity())
                .ok_or("Animation size overflow")?;
            let expected = input
                .count()
                .checked_mul(components)
                .and_then(|n| {
                    n.checked_mul(if interpolation == Interpolation::CubicSpline {
                        3
                    } else {
                        1
                    })
                })
                .ok_or("Animation size overflow")?;
            if components == 0 || output_scalars != expected {
                return Err("Invalid animation output count/data".into());
            }
            expanded_animation_scalars = expanded_animation_scalars
                .checked_add(input.count())
                .and_then(|n| n.checked_add(output_scalars))
                .ok_or("Animation size overflow")?;
            if expanded_animation_scalars > 16 * 1024 * 1024 {
                return Err("Expanded animation data exceeds memory limit".into());
            }
            let reader = channel.reader(|b| buffers.get(b.index()).copied());
            let times: Vec<_> = reader
                .read_inputs()
                .ok_or("Cannot read animation timestamps")?
                .collect();
            if times.is_empty()
                || times.iter().any(|t| !t.is_finite() || *t < 0.)
                || times.windows(2).any(|w| w[1] <= w[0])
            {
                return Err("Animation timestamps must be finite and strictly increasing".into());
            }
            let values: Vec<f32> = match reader
                .read_outputs()
                .ok_or("Cannot read animation output")?
            {
                gltf::animation::util::ReadOutputs::Translations(v)
                | gltf::animation::util::ReadOutputs::Scales(v) => v.flatten().collect(),
                gltf::animation::util::ReadOutputs::Rotations(v) => {
                    v.into_f32().flatten().collect()
                }
                gltf::animation::util::ReadOutputs::MorphTargetWeights(v) => v.into_f32().collect(),
            };
            if values.len() != expected || values.iter().any(|v| !v.is_finite()) {
                return Err("Invalid animation output count/data".into());
            }
            if property == AnimationProperty::Rotation {
                let width = components
                    * if interpolation == Interpolation::CubicSpline {
                        3
                    } else {
                        1
                    };
                for key in values.chunks_exact(width) {
                    let value = if interpolation == Interpolation::CubicSpline {
                        &key[4..8]
                    } else {
                        key
                    };
                    let length = value.iter().map(|v| v * v).sum::<f32>();
                    if (length - 1.).abs() > 0.001 {
                        return Err("Animation rotation key must be a unit quaternion".into());
                    }
                }
            }
            clip.duration = clip.duration.max(*times.last().unwrap());
            clip.channels.push(AnimationChannel {
                node,
                property,
                interpolation,
                times,
                values,
                components,
            });
        }
        asset.animations.push(clip);
    }
    for scene in gltf.scenes() {
        asset.scenes.push(ModelScene {
            name: scene.name().map(str::to_owned),
            nodes: scene.nodes().map(|n| n.index()).collect(),
        });
    }
    for camera in gltf.cameras() {
        match camera.projection() {
            gltf::camera::Projection::Perspective(p) => {
                if !p.yfov().is_finite()
                    || p.yfov() <= 0.
                    || p.yfov() >= std::f32::consts::PI
                    || !p.znear().is_finite()
                    || p.znear() <= 0.
                    || p.zfar()
                        .is_some_and(|far| !far.is_finite() || far <= p.znear())
                    || p.aspect_ratio()
                        .is_some_and(|ratio| !ratio.is_finite() || ratio <= 0.)
                {
                    return Err("Invalid perspective camera parameters".into());
                }
            }
            gltf::camera::Projection::Orthographic(o) => {
                if [o.xmag(), o.ymag(), o.znear(), o.zfar()]
                    .iter()
                    .any(|v| !v.is_finite())
                    || o.xmag() <= 0.
                    || o.ymag() <= 0.
                    || o.znear() < 0.
                    || o.zfar() <= o.znear()
                {
                    return Err("Invalid orthographic camera parameters".into());
                }
            }
        }
        asset.cameras.push(match camera.projection() {
            gltf::camera::Projection::Perspective(p) => ModelCamera::Perspective {
                name: camera.name().map(str::to_owned),
                yfov: p.yfov(),
                aspect_ratio: p.aspect_ratio(),
                znear: p.znear(),
                zfar: p.zfar(),
            },
            gltf::camera::Projection::Orthographic(o) => ModelCamera::Orthographic {
                name: camera.name().map(str::to_owned),
                xmag: o.xmag(),
                ymag: o.ymag(),
                znear: o.znear(),
                zfar: o.zfar(),
            },
        });
    }
    // Validate graph/skin relationships even when no scene is selected for rendering.
    crate::gltf_animation::rest_pose(&asset)?;
    let children: std::collections::HashSet<_> = asset
        .nodes
        .iter()
        .flat_map(|n| n.children.iter().copied())
        .collect();
    if asset.scenes.is_empty() {
        asset.scenes.push(ModelScene {
            name: None,
            nodes: (0..asset.nodes.len())
                .filter(|node| !children.contains(node))
                .collect(),
        });
        asset.default_scene = Some(0);
    }
    for scene in &asset.scenes {
        let mut roots = std::collections::HashSet::new();
        if scene
            .nodes
            .iter()
            .any(|&node| children.contains(&node) || !roots.insert(node))
        {
            return Err("Scene roots must be distinct nodes without parents".into());
        }
    }
    Ok(asset)
}
fn wrap_mode(mode: gltf::texture::WrappingMode) -> crate::gltf_model::WrapMode {
    match mode {
        gltf::texture::WrappingMode::ClampToEdge => crate::gltf_model::WrapMode::ClampToEdge,
        gltf::texture::WrappingMode::MirroredRepeat => crate::gltf_model::WrapMode::MirroredRepeat,
        gltf::texture::WrappingMode::Repeat => crate::gltf_model::WrapMode::Repeat,
    }
}
fn decode_core_image(bytes: &[u8], mime: Option<&str>) -> Result<(u32, u32, Vec<u8>), String> {
    use std::io::Cursor;
    let format = image::guess_format(bytes).map_err(|e| format!("Invalid glTF image: {e}"))?;
    if !matches!(format, image::ImageFormat::Png | image::ImageFormat::Jpeg) {
        return Err("glTF core images must be PNG or JPEG".into());
    }
    if mime.is_some_and(|m| {
        m != if format == image::ImageFormat::Png {
            "image/png"
        } else {
            "image/jpeg"
        }
    }) {
        return Err("Image MIME type does not match encoded image".into());
    }
    let mut reader = image::ImageReader::with_format(Cursor::new(bytes), format);
    let mut limits = image::Limits::default();
    limits.max_image_width = Some(16384);
    limits.max_image_height = Some(16384);
    limits.max_alloc = Some(64 * 1024 * 1024);
    reader.limits(limits);
    let decoded = reader
        .decode()
        .map_err(|e| format!("Cannot decode glTF image: {e}"))?;
    if u64::from(decoded.width()) * u64::from(decoded.height()) > 16_777_216 {
        return Err("glTF image pixel limit exceeded".into());
    }
    Ok((
        decoded.width(),
        decoded.height(),
        decoded.to_rgba8().into_raw(),
    ))
}
fn validate_core_mode(mode: crate::gltf_model::PrimitiveMode, count: usize) -> Result<(), String> {
    use crate::gltf_model::PrimitiveMode::*;
    let valid = match mode {
        Points => count >= 1,
        Lines => count >= 2 && count.is_multiple_of(2),
        LineLoop | LineStrip => count >= 2,
        Triangles => count >= 3 && count.is_multiple_of(3),
        TriangleStrip | TriangleFan => count >= 3,
    };
    if valid {
        Ok(())
    } else {
        Err("Invalid glTF primitive element count".into())
    }
}
pub fn triangle_indices(indices: &[u32], mode: crate::gltf_model::PrimitiveMode) -> Vec<u32> {
    use crate::gltf_model::PrimitiveMode::*;
    match mode {
        Triangles => indices.to_vec(),
        TriangleStrip => indices
            .windows(3)
            .enumerate()
            .flat_map(|(i, t)| {
                if i % 2 == 0 {
                    [t[0], t[1], t[2]]
                } else {
                    [t[1], t[0], t[2]]
                }
            })
            .collect(),
        TriangleFan => indices[1..]
            .windows(2)
            .flat_map(|t| [indices[0], t[0], t[1]])
            .collect(),
        _ => vec![],
    }
}
fn derive_core_normals(
    positions: &[[f32; 3]],
    indices: &[u32],
    mode: crate::gltf_model::PrimitiveMode,
) -> Vec<[f32; 3]> {
    let mut normals = vec![Vec3::ZERO; positions.len()];
    for tri in triangle_indices(indices, mode).as_chunks::<3>().0 {
        let a = Vec3::from_array(positions[tri[0] as usize]);
        let face = (Vec3::from_array(positions[tri[1] as usize]) - a)
            .cross(Vec3::from_array(positions[tri[2] as usize]) - a);
        for &i in tri {
            normals[i as usize] += face;
        }
    }
    normals
        .into_iter()
        .map(|n| n.try_normalize().unwrap_or(Vec3::Y).to_array())
        .collect()
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod core_tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::{gltf_animation::*, gltf_model::*};
    use serde_json::{json, Value};
    use std::sync::Arc;
    fn floats(values: &[f32]) -> Vec<u8> {
        values.iter().flat_map(|v| v.to_le_bytes()).collect()
    }
    fn add_accessor(
        doc: &mut Value,
        bytes: &mut Vec<u8>,
        data: &[u8],
        component: u32,
        count: usize,
        kind: &str,
    ) -> usize {
        while !bytes.len().is_multiple_of(4) {
            bytes.push(0);
        }
        let view = doc["bufferViews"].as_array().unwrap().len();
        let offset = bytes.len();
        bytes.extend_from_slice(data);
        doc["bufferViews"]
            .as_array_mut()
            .unwrap()
            .push(json!({"buffer":0,"byteOffset":offset,"byteLength":data.len()}));
        let index = doc["accessors"].as_array().unwrap().len();
        let mut accessor =
            json!({"bufferView":view,"componentType":component,"count":count,"type":kind});
        if index == 0 {
            accessor["min"] = json!([0, 0, 0]);
            accessor["max"] = json!([1, 1, 0]);
        }
        doc["accessors"].as_array_mut().unwrap().push(accessor);
        index
    }
    fn fixture() -> (Value, Vec<u8>) {
        let mut doc = json!({"asset":{"version":"2.0"},"buffers":[{"uri":"a.bin","byteLength":36}],"bufferViews":[],"accessors":[],"meshes":[{"primitives":[{"attributes":{"POSITION":0}}]}],"nodes":[{"mesh":0}],"scenes":[{"nodes":[0]}]});
        let mut bytes = vec![];
        add_accessor(
            &mut doc,
            &mut bytes,
            &floats(&[0., 0., 0., 1., 0., 0., 0., 1., 0.]),
            5126,
            3,
            "VEC3",
        );
        (doc, bytes)
    }
    fn parse_fixture(
        mut doc: Value,
        bytes: Vec<u8>,
        images: Vec<Vec<u8>>,
    ) -> Result<ModelAsset, String> {
        doc["buffers"][0]["byteLength"] = json!(bytes.len());
        parse_model(&serde_json::to_vec(&doc).unwrap(), &[bytes], &images)
    }
    #[test]
    fn shared_morph_weights_and_invalid_pose_lengths_are_rejected() {
        let (doc, bytes) = fixture();
        let mut asset = parse_fixture(doc, bytes, vec![]).unwrap();
        let mut pose = rest_pose(&asset).unwrap();
        pose.world_matrices.clear();
        assert!(recompute_world(&asset, &mut pose)
            .unwrap_err()
            .contains("node count mismatch"));
        asset.meshes[0].weights = vec![0.; 1024];
        asset.nodes = vec![asset.nodes[0].clone(); 20_000];
        assert!(rest_pose(&asset)
            .unwrap_err()
            .contains("pose exceeds memory limit"));
    }
    #[test]
    fn excessive_shared_skin_pose_is_rejected_before_expansion() {
        let (mut doc, bytes) = fixture();
        doc["nodes"] = json!((0..5000).map(|_| json!({"skin":0})).collect::<Vec<_>>());
        doc["skins"] = json!([{"joints":(0..5000).collect::<Vec<_>>()}]);
        let error = parse_fixture(doc, bytes, vec![]).unwrap_err();
        assert!(error.contains("pose exceeds memory limit"), "{error}");
    }
    #[test]
    fn shared_animation_samplers_have_an_expanded_memory_budget() {
        let (mut doc, mut bytes) = fixture();
        let keys = 4096;
        let times = add_accessor(
            &mut doc,
            &mut bytes,
            &floats(&(0..keys).map(|i| i as f32).collect::<Vec<_>>()),
            5126,
            keys,
            "SCALAR",
        );
        let values = add_accessor(
            &mut doc,
            &mut bytes,
            &floats(&vec![0.; keys * 3]),
            5126,
            keys,
            "VEC3",
        );
        doc["nodes"] = json!((0..1100).map(|_| json!({})).collect::<Vec<_>>());
        doc["animations"] = json!([{"samplers":[{"input":times,"output":values}],"channels":(0..1100).map(|node| json!({"sampler":0,"target":{"node":node,"path":"translation"}})).collect::<Vec<_>>()}]);
        let error = parse_fixture(doc, bytes, vec![]).unwrap_err();
        assert!(
            error.contains("animation data exceeds memory limit"),
            "{error}"
        );
    }
    #[test]
    fn texture_coordinate_indices_are_bounded_before_wasm_conversion() {
        for set in [31u32, 32, u32::MAX] {
            let (mut doc, mut bytes) = fixture();
            let uv = add_accessor(&mut doc, &mut bytes, &floats(&[0.; 6]), 5126, 3, "VEC2");
            doc["meshes"][0]["primitives"][0]["attributes"][format!("TEXCOORD_{set}")] = json!(uv);
            let result = parse_fixture(doc, bytes, vec![]);
            if set == 31 {
                assert!(result.is_ok());
            } else {
                assert!(result.unwrap_err().contains("coordinate set limit"));
            }
        }
    }
    #[test]
    fn real_fox_skin_animation_and_helmet_pbr_images() {
        let fox = parse_model(include_bytes!("../assets/models/fox/Fox.glb"), &[], &[]).unwrap();
        assert!(!fox.skins.is_empty());
        assert!(fox.animations.len() >= 3);
        assert!(!fox.images.is_empty());
        let mut instance = ModelInstance::new(Arc::new(fox), None).unwrap();
        let before = model_bounds(&instance.asset, &instance.pose).unwrap();
        instance.play(0, true).unwrap();
        instance.seek(0.5).unwrap();
        let after = model_bounds(&instance.asset, &instance.pose).unwrap();
        assert_ne!(before, after);
        assert!(after.0.is_finite() && after.1.is_finite());
        let helmet = parse_model(
            include_bytes!("../assets/models/damaged-helmet/DamagedHelmet.glb"),
            &[],
            &[],
        )
        .unwrap();
        let m = &helmet.materials[0];
        assert!(m.base_color_texture.is_some());
        assert!(m.metallic_roughness_texture.is_some());
        assert!(m.normal_texture.is_some());
        assert!(m.occlusion_texture.is_some());
        assert!(m.emissive_texture.is_some());
        assert_eq!(helmet.images.len(), 5);
        assert!(helmet.vertices.iter().all(|v| !v.tex_coords.is_empty()));
    }
    #[test]
    fn all_core_modes_and_external_png_jpeg_material_uv_sets() {
        for mode in 0..=6 {
            let (mut doc, mut bytes) = fixture();
            if mode == 1 {
                let accessor = add_accessor(&mut doc, &mut bytes, &[0, 1], 5121, 2, "SCALAR");
                doc["meshes"][0]["primitives"][0]["indices"] = json!(accessor);
            }
            doc["meshes"][0]["primitives"][0]["mode"] = json!(mode);
            assert!(parse_fixture(doc, bytes, vec![]).is_ok());
        }
        for format in [image::ImageFormat::Png, image::ImageFormat::Jpeg] {
            let (mut doc, mut bytes) = fixture();
            let uv = add_accessor(
                &mut doc,
                &mut bytes,
                &floats(&[0., 0., 1., 0., 0., 1.]),
                5126,
                3,
                "VEC2",
            );
            for set in 0..3 {
                doc["meshes"][0]["primitives"][0]["attributes"][format!("TEXCOORD_{set}")] =
                    json!(uv);
            }
            doc["meshes"][0]["primitives"][0]["material"] = json!(0);
            doc["materials"] = json!([{"pbrMetallicRoughness":{"baseColorFactor":[0.25,0.5,0.75,0.6],"metallicFactor":0.4,"roughnessFactor":0.7,"baseColorTexture":{"index":0,"texCoord":2}},"alphaMode":"BLEND","doubleSided":true}]);
            doc["images"] = json!([{"uri":"image.bin"}]);
            doc["textures"] = json!([{"source":0,"sampler":0}]);
            doc["samplers"] =
                json!([{"wrapS":33071,"wrapT":33648,"minFilter":9987,"magFilter":9728}]);
            let image = image::DynamicImage::ImageRgb8(image::RgbImage::from_pixel(
                2,
                1,
                image::Rgb([12, 34, 56]),
            ));
            let mut cursor = std::io::Cursor::new(vec![]);
            image.write_to(&mut cursor, format).unwrap();
            let asset = parse_fixture(doc, bytes, vec![cursor.into_inner()]).unwrap();
            assert_eq!(asset.images[0].rgba.len(), 8);
            assert_eq!(asset.vertices[0].tex_coords.len(), 3);
            assert_eq!(asset.materials[0].alpha_mode, AlphaMode::Blend);
            assert!(asset.materials[0].double_sided);
            assert_eq!(asset.materials[0].base_color_texture.unwrap().tex_coord, 2);
            assert_eq!(asset.samplers[0].wrap_s, WrapMode::ClampToEdge);
            assert_eq!(asset.samplers[0].wrap_t, WrapMode::MirroredRepeat);
        }
    }
    #[test]
    fn sparse_positions_without_base_and_invalid_sparse_indices() {
        let (mut doc, mut bytes) = fixture();
        let indices = add_accessor(&mut doc, &mut bytes, &[0, 1, 2], 5121, 3, "SCALAR");
        let index_view = doc["accessors"][indices]["bufferView"].clone();
        doc["accessors"][0]
            .as_object_mut()
            .unwrap()
            .remove("bufferView");
        doc["accessors"][0]["sparse"] = json!({"count":3,"indices":{"bufferView":index_view,"componentType":5121},"values":{"bufferView":0}});
        let asset = parse_fixture(doc.clone(), bytes.clone(), vec![]).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(asset.vertices[1].position, [1., 0., 0.]);
        let offset = doc["bufferViews"][1]["byteOffset"].as_u64().unwrap() as usize;
        bytes[offset + 1] = 0;
        assert!(parse_fixture(doc, bytes, vec![])
            .unwrap_err()
            .contains("Sparse indices"));
    }
    #[test]
    fn all_joint_sets_morph_then_skin_and_singular_skin_fallback() {
        let (mut doc, mut bytes) = fixture();
        let joints0 = add_accessor(&mut doc, &mut bytes, &[0u8; 12], 5121, 3, "VEC4");
        let weights0 = add_accessor(
            &mut doc,
            &mut bytes,
            &floats(&[1., 0., 0., 0., 1., 0., 0., 0., 1., 0., 0., 0.]),
            5126,
            3,
            "VEC4",
        );
        let joints1 = add_accessor(&mut doc, &mut bytes, &[1u8; 12], 5121, 3, "VEC4");
        let weights1 = add_accessor(
            &mut doc,
            &mut bytes,
            &floats(&[1., 0., 0., 0., 1., 0., 0., 0., 1., 0., 0., 0.]),
            5126,
            3,
            "VEC4",
        );
        let morph = add_accessor(
            &mut doc,
            &mut bytes,
            &floats(&[1., 0., 0., 1., 0., 0., 1., 0., 0.]),
            5126,
            3,
            "VEC3",
        );
        doc["meshes"][0]["primitives"][0]["attributes"]["JOINTS_0"] = json!(joints0);
        doc["meshes"][0]["primitives"][0]["attributes"]["WEIGHTS_0"] = json!(weights0);
        doc["meshes"][0]["primitives"][0]["attributes"]["JOINTS_1"] = json!(joints1);
        doc["meshes"][0]["primitives"][0]["attributes"]["WEIGHTS_1"] = json!(weights1);
        doc["meshes"][0]["primitives"][0]["targets"] = json!([{"POSITION":morph}]);
        doc["meshes"][0]["weights"] = json!([0.5]);
        doc["nodes"] = json!([{"mesh":0,"skin":0},{"translation":[2,0,0]},{"translation":[4,0,0]}]);
        doc["skins"] = json!([{"joints":[1,2]}]);
        doc["scenes"][0]["nodes"] = json!([0, 1, 2]);
        let mut asset = parse_fixture(doc, bytes, vec![]).unwrap();
        let pose = default_pose(&asset, 0).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(asset.vertices[0].influences.len(), 8);
        assert_eq!(
            deformed_vertices(&asset, &pose, 0, 0).unwrap()[0].position,
            [3.5, 0., 0.]
        );
        asset.nodes[0].trs.scale = Vec3::ZERO;
        let pose = default_pose(&asset, 0).unwrap();
        assert!(pose.skin_world_space[0]);
        assert_eq!(node_render_matrix(&pose, 0), Mat4::IDENTITY);
        assert_eq!(
            deformed_vertices(&asset, &pose, 0, 0).unwrap()[0].position,
            [3.5, 0., 0.]
        );
    }
    #[test]
    fn model_mutations_and_updates_are_atomic_on_deformation_overflow() {
        let (mut doc, mut bytes) = fixture();
        let morph = add_accessor(
            &mut doc,
            &mut bytes,
            &floats(&[10., 0., 0., 10., 0., 0., 10., 0., 0.]),
            5126,
            3,
            "VEC3",
        );
        doc["meshes"][0]["primitives"][0]["targets"] = json!([{"POSITION":morph}]);
        doc["meshes"][0]["weights"] = json!([0.]);
        let times = add_accessor(&mut doc, &mut bytes, &floats(&[0., 1.]), 5126, 2, "SCALAR");
        let values = add_accessor(
            &mut doc,
            &mut bytes,
            &floats(&[0., f32::MAX]),
            5126,
            2,
            "SCALAR",
        );
        doc["animations"] = json!([{"samplers":[{"input":times,"output":values}],"channels":[{"sampler":0,"target":{"node":0,"path":"weights"}}]}]);
        let asset = Arc::new(parse_fixture(doc, bytes, vec![]).unwrap());
        let mut scene = crate::scene::SceneManager::default();
        let id = scene.add_model(asset).unwrap();
        let revision = scene.model(id).unwrap().revision;
        let render_revision = scene.render_revision();
        assert!(scene.set_model_morph_weights(id, 0, &[f32::MAX]).is_err());
        assert_eq!(scene.model(id).unwrap().revision, revision);
        assert_eq!(scene.render_revision(), render_revision);
        assert_eq!(scene.model(id).unwrap().pose.node_weights[0], [0.]);
        scene.play_model_animation(id, 0, false, 1.).unwrap();
        let revision = scene.model(id).unwrap().revision;
        scene.update(1.);
        let model = scene.model(id).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(model.revision, revision);
        assert_eq!(model.pose.node_weights[0], [0.]);
        assert_eq!(model.playback.as_ref().unwrap().time, 0.);
        assert!(!model.playback.as_ref().unwrap().playing);
        assert!(model.last_error.as_ref().unwrap().contains("overflow"));
    }

        assert!(matches!(
            asset.cameras[0],
            ModelCamera::Perspective { zfar: None, .. }
        ));

        assert!(parse_fixture(doc, bytes, vec![]).is_err());
    }
    #[test]
    fn changing_clips_resets_unanimated_morph_weights_and_marks_flat_normals() {
        let (mut doc, mut bytes) = fixture();
        let morph = add_accessor(
            &mut doc,
            &mut bytes,
            &floats(&[1., 0., 0., 1., 0., 0., 1., 0., 0.]),
            5126,
            3,
            "VEC3",
        );
        doc["meshes"][0]["primitives"][0]["targets"] = json!([{"POSITION":morph}]);
        doc["meshes"][0]["weights"] = json!([0.25]);
        let mut asset = parse_fixture(doc, bytes, vec![]).unwrap();
        assert!(asset.primitives[0].flat_normals);
        assert_eq!(asset.vertices[0].tangent, [0., 0., 0., 1.]);
        asset.animations = vec![
            AnimationClip {
                name: None,
                duration: 1.,
                channels: vec![AnimationChannel {
                    node: 0,
                    property: AnimationProperty::Weights,
                    interpolation: Interpolation::Step,
                    times: vec![0.],
                    values: vec![0.75],
                    components: 1,
                }],
            },
            AnimationClip {
                name: None,
                duration: 1.,
                channels: vec![AnimationChannel {
                    node: 0,
                    property: AnimationProperty::Translation,
                    interpolation: Interpolation::Step,
                    times: vec![0.],
                    values: vec![0., 0., 0.],
                    components: 3,
                }],
            },
        ];
        let mut instance = ModelInstance::new(Arc::new(asset), None).unwrap();
        instance.play(0, false).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(instance.pose.node_weights[0], [0.75]);
        instance.play(1, false).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(instance.pose.node_weights[0], [0.25]);
        instance.set_morph_weights(0, &[0.4]).unwrap();
        instance.update(0.1).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(instance.pose.node_weights[0], [0.4]);
        instance.play(0, false).unwrap();
        instance.update(0.1).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(instance.pose.node_weights[0], [0.75]);
        instance.stop().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(instance.pose.node_weights[0], [0.25]);
        assert!(instance.morph_overrides.is_empty());
        assert_eq!(
            deformed_vertices(&instance.asset, &instance.pose, 0, 0).unwrap()[0].tangent,
            [0., 0., 0., 1.]
        );
    }
    #[test]
    fn imported_animation_and_scene_model_root_lifecycle() {
        let (mut doc, mut bytes) = fixture();
        let times = add_accessor(&mut doc, &mut bytes, &floats(&[0., 2.]), 5126, 2, "SCALAR");
        let values = add_accessor(
            &mut doc,
            &mut bytes,
            &floats(&[0., 0., 0., 4., 0., 0.]),
            5126,
            2,
            "VEC3",
        );
        doc["animations"] = json!([{"samplers":[{"input":times,"output":values,"interpolation":"LINEAR"}],"channels":[{"sampler":0,"target":{"node":0,"path":"translation"}}]}]);
        let asset = Arc::new(parse_fixture(doc, bytes, vec![]).unwrap());
        let mut scene = crate::scene::SceneManager::default();
        let id = scene.add_model(asset).unwrap();
        scene.play_model_animation(id, 0, false, 1.).unwrap();
        let revision = scene.model(id).unwrap().revision;
        scene.update(1.);
        assert!(scene.model(id).unwrap().revision > revision);
        assert_eq!(
            scene.model(id).unwrap().pose.local_trs[0].translation,
            Vec3::new(2., 0., 0.)
        );
        scene.pause_model_animation(id, true).unwrap();
        let revision = scene.model(id).unwrap().revision;
        scene.update(1.);
        assert_eq!(scene.model(id).unwrap().revision, revision);
        scene.seek_model_animation(id, 0.5).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(scene.model(id).unwrap().pose.local_trs[0].translation.x, 1.);
        scene.stop_model_animation(id).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            scene.model(id).unwrap().pose.local_trs[0].translation,
            Vec3::ZERO
        );
        scene.remove_object(id);
        assert!(scene.model(id).is_none());
        assert!(scene.play_model_animation(id, 0, false, 1.).is_err());
    }
}

\n# File: src/gltf_model.rs
//! CPU representation of glTF 2.0 core. All indices address the containing asset.
use glam::{Mat4, Quat, Vec3};
use std::ops::Range;

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct ModelVertex {
    pub position: [f32; 3],
    pub normal: [f32; 3],
    pub tangent: [f32; 4],
    pub uv0: [f32; 2],
    pub uv1: [f32; 2],
    pub color: [f32; 4],
    pub joints: [u16; 4],
    pub weights: [f32; 4],
    /// All JOINTS_n/WEIGHTS_n sets, including sets beyond zero.
    pub influences: Vec<(u16, f32)>,
    pub tex_coords: Vec<[f32; 2]>,
}
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum PrimitiveMode {
    Points,
    Lines,
    LineLoop,
    LineStrip,
    Triangles,
    TriangleStrip,
    TriangleFan,
}
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct MorphTarget {
    pub positions: Vec<[f32; 3]>,
    pub normals: Vec<[f32; 3]>,
    pub tangents: Vec<[f32; 3]>,
}
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct ModelPrimitive {
    /// glTF requires geometric face normals when NORMAL is absent.
    pub flat_normals: bool,
    /// Slice in ModelAsset.vertices. Indices are LOCAL to this slice.
    pub vertices: Range<usize>,
    pub indices: Vec<u32>,
    pub mode: PrimitiveMode,
    pub material: Option<usize>,
    pub morph_targets: Vec<MorphTarget>,
}
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct ModelMesh {
    pub name: Option<String>,
    pub primitives: Vec<usize>,
    pub weights: Vec<f32>,
}
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum AlphaMode {
    Opaque,
    Mask,
    Blend,
}
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
pub struct TextureSlot {
    pub texture: usize,
    pub tex_coord: u32,
}
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct ModelMaterial {
    pub name: Option<String>,
    pub base_color_factor: [f32; 4],
    pub metallic_factor: f32,
    pub roughness_factor: f32,
    pub emissive_factor: [f32; 3],
    pub base_color_texture: Option<TextureSlot>,
    pub metallic_roughness_texture: Option<TextureSlot>,
    pub normal_texture: Option<TextureSlot>,
    pub normal_scale: f32,
    pub occlusion_texture: Option<TextureSlot>,
    pub occlusion_strength: f32,
    pub emissive_texture: Option<TextureSlot>,
    pub alpha_mode: AlphaMode,
    pub alpha_cutoff: f32,
    pub double_sided: bool,
}
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct ModelImage {
    pub name: Option<String>,
    pub width: u32,
    pub height: u32,
    pub rgba: Vec<u8>,
}
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct ModelTexture {
    pub image: usize,
    pub sampler: Option<usize>,
}
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum WrapMode {
    ClampToEdge,
    MirroredRepeat,
    Repeat,
}
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum FilterMode {
    Nearest,
    Linear,
    NearestMipmapNearest,
    LinearMipmapNearest,
    NearestMipmapLinear,
    LinearMipmapLinear,
}
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct ModelSampler {
    pub wrap_s: WrapMode,
    pub wrap_t: WrapMode,
    pub mag_filter: Option<FilterMode>,
    pub min_filter: Option<FilterMode>,
}
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
pub struct NodeTrs {
    pub translation: Vec3,
    pub rotation: Quat,
    pub scale: Vec3,
}
impl NodeTrs {
    pub fn matrix(self) -> Mat4 {
        Mat4::from_scale_rotation_translation(self.scale, self.rotation, self.translation)
    }
}
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct ModelNode {
    pub name: Option<String>,
    pub children: Vec<usize>,
    pub mesh: Option<usize>,
    pub skin: Option<usize>,
    pub camera: Option<usize>,
    pub trs: NodeTrs,
    /// Matrix-authored nodes cannot be targets of TRS animation channels.
    pub matrix: Option<Mat4>,
    pub weights: Option<Vec<f32>>,
}
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct ModelSkin {
    pub name: Option<String>,
    pub joints: Vec<usize>,
    pub inverse_bind_matrices: Vec<Mat4>,
    pub skeleton: Option<usize>,
}
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub enum ModelCamera {
    Perspective {
        name: Option<String>,
        yfov: f32,
        aspect_ratio: Option<f32>,
        znear: f32,
        zfar: Option<f32>,
    },
    Orthographic {
        name: Option<String>,
        xmag: f32,
        ymag: f32,
        znear: f32,
        zfar: f32,
    },
}
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Interpolation {
    Step,
    Linear,
    CubicSpline,
}
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum AnimationProperty {
    Translation,
    Rotation,
    Scale,
    Weights,
}
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct AnimationChannel {
    pub node: usize,
    pub property: AnimationProperty,
    pub interpolation: Interpolation,
    pub times: Vec<f32>,
    /// Flat sample scalars. For CUBICSPLINE each key is [in tangent, value, out tangent].
    pub values: Vec<f32>,
    pub components: usize,
}
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct AnimationClip {
    pub name: Option<String>,
    pub channels: Vec<AnimationChannel>,
    pub duration: f32,
}
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct ModelScene {
    pub name: Option<String>,
    pub nodes: Vec<usize>,
}
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct ModelAsset {
    pub vertices: Vec<ModelVertex>,
    pub primitives: Vec<ModelPrimitive>,
    pub meshes: Vec<ModelMesh>,
    pub materials: Vec<ModelMaterial>,
    pub images: Vec<ModelImage>,
    pub textures: Vec<ModelTexture>,
    pub samplers: Vec<ModelSampler>,
    pub nodes: Vec<ModelNode>,
    pub skins: Vec<ModelSkin>,
    pub animations: Vec<AnimationClip>,
    pub scenes: Vec<ModelScene>,
    pub default_scene: Option<usize>,
    pub cameras: Vec<ModelCamera>,
    pub warnings: Vec<String>,
}
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct ModelPose {
    pub local_trs: Vec<NodeTrs>,
    pub world_matrices: Vec<Mat4>,
    pub node_weights: Vec<Vec<f32>>,
    /// Per mesh NODE, already in that node's local space: inverse(meshWorld)*jointWorld*IBM.
    pub skin_matrices: Vec<Vec<Mat4>>,
    /// Singular mesh transforms use world-space skin deformation instead of an inverse.
    pub skin_world_space: Vec<bool>,
    /// Nodes reachable from the selected scene; joint matrices may use other nodes.
    pub active_nodes: Vec<usize>,
}
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct AnimationPlayback {
    pub clip: usize,
    pub time: f32,
    pub speed: f32,
    pub looping: bool,
    pub playing: bool,
}
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct ModelInstance {
    pub asset: std::sync::Arc<ModelAsset>,
    pub pose: ModelPose,
    pub playback: Option<AnimationPlayback>,
    pub scene: usize,
    pub revision: u64,
    pub last_error: Option<String>,
    /// Manual weights persist for properties not targeted by the active animation.
    pub morph_overrides: std::collections::BTreeMap<usize, Vec<f32>>,
}

\n# File: src/input/active_camera.rs
use crate::input::camera::{Camera, CameraController};
use crate::input::orbit_camera::OrbitCamera;
use glam::{Mat4, Vec3};

#[derive(Copy, Clone)]
pub enum CameraType {
    Free,
    Orbit,
}

pub struct ActiveCamera {
    free: Camera,
    orbit: OrbitCamera,
    active: CameraType,
}

impl ActiveCamera {
    pub fn new(aspect: f32) -> Self {
        Self {
            free: Camera::new(aspect),
            orbit: OrbitCamera::new(aspect),
            active: CameraType::Orbit,
        }
    }

    pub fn set_type(&mut self, ty: CameraType) {
        self.clear_input();
        self.active = ty;
    }

    pub fn set_view(&mut self, position: Vec3, target: Vec3) -> bool {
        if !self.free.set_view(position, target) {
            return false;
        }
        self.set_type(CameraType::Free);
        true
    }

    pub fn set_aspect(&mut self, aspect: f32) {
        self.free.set_aspect(aspect);
        self.orbit.set_aspect(aspect);
    }

    fn active_mut(&mut self) -> &mut dyn CameraController {
        match self.active {
            CameraType::Free => &mut self.free,
            CameraType::Orbit => &mut self.orbit,
        }
    }

    fn active_ref(&self) -> &dyn CameraController {
        match self.active {
            CameraType::Free => &self.free,
            CameraType::Orbit => &self.orbit,
        }
    }
}

impl CameraController for ActiveCamera {
    fn clear_input(&mut self) {
        self.free.clear_input();
        self.orbit.clear_input();
    }

    fn key_down(&mut self, code: String) {
        self.active_mut().key_down(code);
    }

    fn key_up(&mut self, code: String) {
        self.active_mut().key_up(code);
    }

    fn mouse_move(&mut self, dx: f32, dy: f32) {
        self.active_mut().mouse_move(dx, dy);
    }

    fn update(&mut self, dt: f32) {
        self.active_mut().update(dt);
    }

    fn matrix(&self) -> Mat4 {
        self.active_ref().matrix()
    }

    fn position(&self) -> Vec3 {
        self.active_ref().position()
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn explicit_free_view_projects_target_and_rejects_invalid_input() {
        let mut camera = ActiveCamera::new(1.0);
        let position = Vec3::new(12.0, 3.0, 0.0);
        let target = Vec3::new(-8.0, 5.0, 0.0);
        assert!(camera.set_view(position, target));
        assert_eq!(camera.position(), position);
        let clip = camera.matrix() * target.extend(1.0);
        assert!(clip.x.abs() < 1e-5 && clip.y.abs() < 1e-5 && clip.w > 0.0);
        let previous = camera.matrix();
        for bad in [position, Vec3::splat(f32::NAN), position + Vec3::Y] {
            assert!(!camera.set_view(position, bad));
            assert_eq!(camera.matrix(), previous);
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn switching_camera_releases_held_keys() {
        let mut camera = ActiveCamera::new(16.0 / 9.0);
        camera.set_type(CameraType::Free);
        camera.key_down("KeyW".into());
        camera.update(0.5);
        let position = camera.position();
        camera.set_type(CameraType::Orbit);
        camera.key_up("KeyW".into());
        camera.set_type(CameraType::Free);
        camera.update(0.5);
        assert_eq!(camera.position(), position);
    }

    #[test]
    fn clearing_input_stops_movement_in_both_modes() {
        for mode in [CameraType::Free, CameraType::Orbit] {
            let mut camera = ActiveCamera::new(16.0 / 9.0);
            camera.set_type(mode);
            camera.key_down("KeyW".into());
            let before = camera.position();
            camera.update(0.25);
            assert_ne!(camera.position(), before);
            camera.clear_input();
            let stopped = camera.position();
            camera.update(1.0);
            assert_eq!(camera.position(), stopped);
            assert!(camera.matrix().is_finite());
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn camera_projects_target_in_webgpu_depth_range() {
        let camera = ActiveCamera::new(16.0 / 9.0);
        let target = camera.matrix().project_point3(Vec3::ZERO);
        assert!(target.x.abs() < 1e-5 && target.y.abs() < 1e-5);
        assert!((0.0..1.0).contains(&target.z));
    }
}

\n# File: src/input/camera.rs
use glam::{Mat4, Vec3};
use std::collections::HashSet;

pub trait CameraController {
    fn key_down(&mut self, code: String);
    fn key_up(&mut self, code: String);
    fn mouse_move(&mut self, dx: f32, dy: f32);
    fn clear_input(&mut self);

    fn update(&mut self, dt: f32);
    fn matrix(&self) -> Mat4;
    fn position(&self) -> Vec3;
}

pub struct Camera {
    pub position: Vec3,
    yaw: f32,
    pitch: f32,
    pub speed: f32,
    pub sensitivity: f32,
    pressed: HashSet<String>,
    aspect: f32,
}

impl Camera {
    pub fn new(aspect: f32) -> Self {
        Self {
            position: Vec3::new(5.0, 3.0, 5.0),
            yaw: -3.0 * std::f32::consts::FRAC_PI_4,
            pitch: -0.4,
            speed: 2.0,
            sensitivity: 0.002,
            pressed: HashSet::new(),
            aspect,
        }
    }

    /// Configure a free-camera view. All orientation calculations remain in Rust.
    pub fn set_view(&mut self, position: Vec3, target: Vec3) -> bool {
        let direction = target - position;
        if !position.is_finite()
            || !target.is_finite()
            || !direction.is_finite()
            || direction.length_squared() < 1e-8
            || !direction.length_squared().is_finite()
        {
            return false;
        }
        let forward = direction.normalize();
        if forward.x * forward.x + forward.z * forward.z < 1e-4 {
            return false;
        }
        self.position = position;
        self.yaw = forward.z.atan2(forward.x);
        self.pitch = forward.y.asin();
        self.pressed.clear();
        true
    }

    pub fn set_aspect(&mut self, aspect: f32) {
        self.aspect = aspect;
    }

    pub fn key_down(&mut self, code: String) {
        self.pressed.insert(code);
    }

    pub fn key_up(&mut self, code: String) {
        self.pressed.remove(&code);
    }

    pub fn mouse_move(&mut self, dx: f32, dy: f32) {
        self.yaw -= dx * self.sensitivity;
        self.pitch = (self.pitch + dy * self.sensitivity).clamp(-1.54, 1.54);
    }

    pub fn update(&mut self, dt: f32) {
        let speed = self.speed
            * if self.pressed.contains("ShiftLeft") || self.pressed.contains("ShiftRight") {
                4.0
            } else {
                1.0
            };
        let forward = self.forward();
        let right = Vec3::Y.cross(forward).normalize();
        if self.pressed.contains("KeyW") {
            self.position += forward * speed * dt;
        }
        if self.pressed.contains("KeyS") {
            self.position -= forward * speed * dt;
        }
        if self.pressed.contains("KeyA") {
            self.position -= right * speed * dt;
        }
        if self.pressed.contains("KeyD") {
            self.position += right * speed * dt;
        }
        if self.pressed.contains("KeyE") {
            self.position.y += speed * dt;
        }
        if self.pressed.contains("KeyQ") {
            self.position.y -= speed * dt;
        }
    }

    pub fn matrix(&self) -> Mat4 {
        let view = glam::camera::lh::view::look_at_mat4(
            self.position,
            self.position + self.forward(),
            Vec3::Y,
        );
        let proj = glam::camera::lh::proj::directx::perspective(
            std::f32::consts::FRAC_PI_4,
            self.aspect,
            0.1,
            100.0,
        );
        proj * view
    }

    fn forward(&self) -> Vec3 {
        Vec3::new(
            self.yaw.cos() * self.pitch.cos(),
            self.pitch.sin(),
            self.yaw.sin() * self.pitch.cos(),
        )
        .normalize()
    }
}

impl CameraController for Camera {
    fn clear_input(&mut self) {
        self.pressed.clear();
    }

    fn key_down(&mut self, code: String) {
        Camera::key_down(self, code);
    }

    fn key_up(&mut self, code: String) {
        Camera::key_up(self, code);
    }

    fn mouse_move(&mut self, dx: f32, dy: f32) {
        Camera::mouse_move(self, dx, dy);
    }

    fn update(&mut self, dt: f32) {
        Camera::update(self, dt);
    }

    fn matrix(&self) -> Mat4 {
        Camera::matrix(self)
    }

    fn position(&self) -> Vec3 {
        self.position
    }
}

\n# File: src/input/keyboard.rs
#![cfg(target_arch = "wasm32")]

use std::{cell::RefCell, rc::Rc};
use wasm_bindgen::{closure::Closure, JsCast};
use web_sys::Window;

use crate::input::camera::CameraController;

pub fn attach<T>(window: &Window, cam: Rc<RefCell<T>>)
where
    T: CameraController + 'static,
{
    let cam_down = cam.clone();
    let key_down = Closure::wrap(Box::new(move |e: web_sys::KeyboardEvent| {
        cam_down.borrow_mut().key_down(e.code());
    }) as Box<dyn FnMut(_)>);
    window
        .add_event_listener_with_callback("keydown", key_down.as_ref().unchecked_ref())
        .unwrap();
    key_down.forget();

    let cam_up = cam.clone();
    let key_up = Closure::wrap(Box::new(move |e: web_sys::KeyboardEvent| {
        cam_up.borrow_mut().key_up(e.code());
    }) as Box<dyn FnMut(_)>);
    window
        .add_event_listener_with_callback("keyup", key_up.as_ref().unchecked_ref())
        .unwrap();
    key_up.forget();

    let on_blur = Closure::wrap(Box::new(move || {
        cam.borrow_mut().clear_input();
    }) as Box<dyn FnMut()>);
    window
        .add_event_listener_with_callback("blur", on_blur.as_ref().unchecked_ref())
        .unwrap();
    on_blur.forget();
}

\n# File: src/input/mouse.rs
#![cfg(target_arch = "wasm32")]

use std::{cell::RefCell, rc::Rc};
use wasm_bindgen::{closure::Closure, JsCast};
use web_sys::{HtmlCanvasElement, PointerEvent, Window};

use crate::input::camera::CameraController;

pub fn attach<T>(window: &Window, canvas: &HtmlCanvasElement, cam: Rc<RefCell<T>>)
where
    T: CameraController + 'static,
{
    let dragging = Rc::new(RefCell::new(false));

    // Start dragging only when the left button is pressed on the canvas
    {
        let dragging = dragging.clone();
        let canvas_clone = canvas.clone();
        let on_down = Closure::wrap(Box::new(move |e: PointerEvent| {
            if e.buttons() & 1 == 1 {
                if let Some(target) = e.target() {
                    if target
                        == canvas_clone
                            .clone()
                            .dyn_into::<web_sys::EventTarget>()
                            .unwrap()
                    {
                        *dragging.borrow_mut() = true;
                    }
                }
            }
        }) as Box<dyn FnMut(_)>);
        canvas
            .add_event_listener_with_callback("pointerdown", on_down.as_ref().unchecked_ref())
            .unwrap();
        on_down.forget();
    }

    // Stop dragging on mouseup anywhere in the window
    {
        let dragging = dragging.clone();
        let on_up = Closure::wrap(Box::new(move |_e: PointerEvent| {
            *dragging.borrow_mut() = false;
        }) as Box<dyn FnMut(_)>);
        window
            .add_event_listener_with_callback("pointerup", on_up.as_ref().unchecked_ref())
            .unwrap();
        on_up.forget();
    }

    // Apply mouse movement only while dragging
    {
        let dragging = dragging.clone();
        let cam_mouse = cam.clone();
        let mouse_move = Closure::wrap(Box::new(move |e: PointerEvent| {
            if *dragging.borrow() && e.buttons() & 1 == 1 {
                cam_mouse
                    .borrow_mut()
                    .mouse_move(e.movement_x() as f32, e.movement_y() as f32);
            }
        }) as Box<dyn FnMut(_)>);
        window
            .add_event_listener_with_callback("pointermove", mouse_move.as_ref().unchecked_ref())
            .unwrap();
        mouse_move.forget();
    }
}

\n# File: src/input/orbit_camera.rs
use glam::{Mat4, Vec3};
use std::collections::HashSet;

pub struct OrbitCamera {
    pub position: Vec3,
    target: Vec3,
    radius: f32,
    yaw: f32,
    pitch: f32,
    pub speed: f32,
    pub sensitivity: f32,
    pressed: HashSet<String>,
    aspect: f32,
}

impl OrbitCamera {
    pub fn new(aspect: f32) -> Self {
        let radius: f32 = 8.0;
        let yaw: f32 = std::f32::consts::FRAC_PI_4;
        let pitch: f32 = 0.35;
        let target = Vec3::ZERO;
        let position = target
            + Vec3::new(
                radius * yaw.cos() * pitch.cos(),
                radius * pitch.sin(),
                radius * yaw.sin() * pitch.cos(),
            );
        Self {
            position,
            target,
            radius,
            yaw,
            pitch,
            speed: 2.0,
            sensitivity: 0.002,
            pressed: HashSet::new(),
            aspect,
        }
    }

    pub fn set_aspect(&mut self, aspect: f32) {
        self.aspect = aspect;
    }

    pub fn key_down(&mut self, code: String) {
        self.pressed.insert(code);
    }

    pub fn key_up(&mut self, code: String) {
        self.pressed.remove(&code);
    }

    pub fn mouse_move(&mut self, dx: f32, dy: f32) {
        self.yaw -= dx * self.sensitivity;
        self.pitch = (self.pitch + dy * self.sensitivity).clamp(-1.54, 1.54);
    }

    pub fn update(&mut self, dt: f32) {
        // Directions relative to the current camera orientation
        let forward = (self.target - self.position).normalize();
        let right = Vec3::Y.cross(forward).normalize();

        if self.pressed.contains("KeyW") {
            self.target += forward * self.speed * dt;
        }
        if self.pressed.contains("KeyS") {
            self.target -= forward * self.speed * dt;
        }
        if self.pressed.contains("KeyA") {
            self.target -= right * self.speed * dt;
        }
        if self.pressed.contains("KeyD") {
            self.target += right * self.speed * dt;
        }
        if self.pressed.contains("Equal") || self.pressed.contains("NumpadAdd") {
            self.radius = (self.radius - self.speed * dt).max(0.5);
        }
        if self.pressed.contains("Minus") || self.pressed.contains("NumpadSubtract") {
            self.radius += self.speed * dt;
        }
        // recalc position
        self.position = self.target
            + Vec3::new(
                self.radius * self.yaw.cos() * self.pitch.cos(),
                self.radius * self.pitch.sin(),
                self.radius * self.yaw.sin() * self.pitch.cos(),
            );
    }

    pub fn matrix(&self) -> Mat4 {
        let view = glam::camera::lh::view::look_at_mat4(self.position, self.target, Vec3::Y);
        let proj = glam::camera::lh::proj::directx::perspective(
            std::f32::consts::FRAC_PI_4,
            self.aspect,
            0.1,
            100.0,
        );
        proj * view
    }
}

use crate::input::camera::CameraController;

impl CameraController for OrbitCamera {
    fn clear_input(&mut self) {
        self.pressed.clear();
    }

    fn key_down(&mut self, code: String) {
        OrbitCamera::key_down(self, code);
    }

    fn key_up(&mut self, code: String) {
        OrbitCamera::key_up(self, code);
    }

    fn mouse_move(&mut self, dx: f32, dy: f32) {
        OrbitCamera::mouse_move(self, dx, dy);
    }

    fn update(&mut self, dt: f32) {
        OrbitCamera::update(self, dt);
    }

    fn matrix(&self) -> Mat4 {
        OrbitCamera::matrix(self)
    }

    fn position(&self) -> Vec3 {
        self.position
    }
}

use wgpu::VertexBufferLayout;
#[repr(C)]
#[derive(Clone, Copy, bytemuck::Pod, bytemuck::Zeroable)]
pub struct Vertex {
    pub position: [f32; 3],
    pub color: [f32; 3],
    pub normal: [f32; 3],
}

impl Vertex {
    pub fn layout<'a>() -> VertexBufferLayout<'a> {
        use std::mem;
        VertexBufferLayout {
            array_stride: mem::size_of::<Vertex>() as wgpu::BufferAddress,
            step_mode: wgpu::VertexStepMode::Vertex,
            attributes: &[
                wgpu::VertexAttribute {
                    offset: 0,
                    shader_location: 0,
                    format: wgpu::VertexFormat::Float32x3,
                },
                wgpu::VertexAttribute {
                    offset: mem::size_of::<[f32; 3]>() as wgpu::BufferAddress,
                    shader_location: 1,
                    format: wgpu::VertexFormat::Float32x3,
                },
                wgpu::VertexAttribute {
                    offset: (mem::size_of::<[f32; 3]>() * 2) as wgpu::BufferAddress,
                    shader_location: 2,
                    format: wgpu::VertexFormat::Float32x3,
                },
            ],
        }
    }
}

pub const INDICES: &[u16] = &[
    0, 1, 2, 0, 2, 3, // front
    4, 5, 6, 4, 6, 7, // back
    8, 9, 10, 8, 10, 11, // left

    20, 21, 22, 20, 22, 23, // bottom

/// Only initialized, padding-free GPU data may be viewed as bytes.
pub fn as_bytes<T: bytemuck::Pod>(data: &[T]) -> &[u8] {
    bytemuck::cast_slice(data)
}

#[repr(C)]
#[derive(Clone, Copy, PartialEq, bytemuck::Pod, bytemuck::Zeroable)]
pub struct Light {
    pub position: [f32; 3],
    pub _pad_p: f32,
    pub color: [f32; 3],
    pub _pad_c: f32,
}

pub use crate::scene::MAX_LIGHTS;
pub const LIGHT_VERTICES_PER_LIGHT: usize = 8 + 3 * 16 * 2;
pub const EMPTY_LIGHT: Light = Light {
    position: [0.0, 0.0, 0.0],
    _pad_p: 0.0,
    color: [0.0, 0.0, 0.0],
    _pad_c: 0.0,
};

#[repr(C)]
#[derive(Clone, Copy, bytemuck::Pod, bytemuck::Zeroable)]
pub struct SceneUniforms {
    pub view_projection: [[f32; 4]; 4],
    pub camera_pos: [f32; 3],
    pub _pad0: f32,
    pub lights: [Light; MAX_LIGHTS],
}

pub fn grid_vertices(size: i32) -> Vec<Vertex> {
    let mut verts = Vec::new();
    let color = [0.3, 0.3, 0.3];
    let normal = [0.0, 1.0, 0.0];
    for i in -size..=size {
        let f = i as f32;
        verts.push(Vertex {
            position: [-size as f32, 0.0, f],
            color,
            normal,
        });
        verts.push(Vertex {
            position: [size as f32, 0.0, f],
            color,
            normal,
        });
        verts.push(Vertex {
            position: [f, 0.0, -size as f32],
            color,
            normal,
        });
        verts.push(Vertex {
            position: [f, 0.0, size as f32],
            color,
            normal,
        });
    }
    verts
}

pub fn light_rays(lights: &[Light]) -> Vec<Vertex> {
    let mut verts = Vec::new();
    let normal = [0.0_f32, 1.0, 0.0];
    let cross = 0.2_f32;
    for l in lights {
        let p = l.position;
        let intensity = l.color.into_iter().fold(0.0_f32, f32::max).max(0.0001);
        let color = l.color.map(|channel| channel / intensity);
        // Three wire rings make the source position visible from every direction.
        for axis in 0..3 {
            for segment in 0..16 {
                for endpoint in [segment, segment + 1] {
                    let angle = endpoint as f32 * std::f32::consts::TAU / 16.0;
                    let mut position = p;
                    position[(axis + 1) % 3] += 0.16 * angle.cos();
                    position[(axis + 2) % 3] += 0.16 * angle.sin();
                    verts.push(Vertex {
                        position,
                        color,
                        normal,
                    });
                }
            }
        }
        // small cross marking the light position
        verts.push(Vertex {
            position: [p[0] - cross, p[1], p[2]],
            color,
            normal,
        });
        verts.push(Vertex {
            position: [p[0] + cross, p[1], p[2]],
            color,
            normal,
        });
        verts.push(Vertex {
            position: [p[0], p[1] - cross, p[2]],
            color,
            normal,
        });
        verts.push(Vertex {
            position: [p[0], p[1] + cross, p[2]],
            color,
            normal,
        });
        verts.push(Vertex {
            position: [p[0], p[1], p[2] - cross],
            color,
            normal,
        });
        verts.push(Vertex {
            position: [p[0], p[1], p[2] + cross],
            color,
            normal,
        });
        // line from light to origin
        verts.push(Vertex {
            position: p,
            color,
            normal,
        });
        verts.push(Vertex {
            position: [0.0, 0.0, 0.0],
            color,
            normal,
        });
    }
    verts
}

pub fn plane_mesh() -> (Vec<Vertex>, Vec<u16>) {
    let color = [0.25, 0.25, 0.28];
    let normal_up = [0.0, 1.0, 0.0];
    let normal_down = [0.0, -1.0, 0.0];
    let s = 0.5;
    let positions = [[-s, 0.0, -s], [s, 0.0, -s], [s, 0.0, s], [-s, 0.0, s]];
    let mut vertices = Vec::with_capacity(8);
    for p in positions {
        vertices.push(Vertex {
            position: p,
            color,
            normal: normal_up,
        });
    }
    for p in positions {
        vertices.push(Vertex {
            position: p,
            color,
            normal: normal_down,
        });
    }
    let indices: Vec<u16> = vec![
        0, 2, 1, 0, 3, 2, // top: winding agrees with +Y normal
        4, 5, 6, 4, 6, 7, // bottom: winding agrees with -Y normal
    ];
    (vertices, indices)
}

pub fn sphere_mesh(segments_u: u32, segments_v: u32) -> (Vec<Vertex>, Vec<u16>) {
    let color = [0.7, 0.7, 0.8];
    let mut vertices = Vec::new();
    let mut indices = Vec::new();
    let u_steps = segments_u.max(3);
    let v_steps = segments_v.max(2);
    for v in 0..=v_steps {
        let v_frac = v as f32 / v_steps as f32;
        let phi = v_frac * std::f32::consts::PI;
        let sin_phi = phi.sin();
        let cos_phi = phi.cos();
        for u in 0..=u_steps {
            let u_frac = u as f32 / u_steps as f32;
            let theta = u_frac * std::f32::consts::TAU;
            let sin_theta = theta.sin();
            let cos_theta = theta.cos();
            let x = sin_phi * cos_theta;
            let y = cos_phi;
            let z = sin_phi * sin_theta;
            vertices.push(Vertex {
                position: [x, y, z],
                color,
                normal: [x, y, z],
            });
        }
    }
    let ring = u_steps + 1;
    for v in 0..v_steps {
        for u in 0..u_steps {
            let i0 = (v * ring + u) as u16;
            let i1 = (v * ring + u + 1) as u16;
            let i2 = ((v + 1) * ring + u) as u16;
            let i3 = ((v + 1) * ring + u + 1) as u16;
            indices.extend_from_slice(&[i0, i1, i2, i1, i3, i2]);
        }
    }
    (vertices, indices)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use glam::Vec3;

    fn assert_winding_matches_normals(vertices: &[Vertex], indices: &[u16]) {
        let mut checked = 0;
        for triangle in indices.as_chunks::<3>().0 {
            let a = vertices[triangle[0] as usize];
            let b = vertices[triangle[1] as usize];
            let c = vertices[triangle[2] as usize];
            let face = (Vec3::from(b.position) - Vec3::from(a.position))
                .cross(Vec3::from(c.position) - Vec3::from(a.position));
            // UV-sphere pole triangles intentionally have zero area.
            if face.length_squared() < 1e-12 {
                continue;
            }
            let normal = Vec3::from(a.normal) + Vec3::from(b.normal) + Vec3::from(c.normal);
            assert!(
                face.dot(normal) > 0.0,
                "triangle {triangle:?} faces against its normals"
            );
            checked += 1;
        }
        assert!(checked > 0);
    }

    #[test]
    fn all_meshes_have_outward_winding_consistent_with_lighting() {
        assert_winding_matches_normals(VERTICES, INDICES);
        let (vertices, indices) = plane_mesh();
        assert_winding_matches_normals(&vertices, &indices);
        let (vertices, indices) = sphere_mesh(32, 16);
        assert_winding_matches_normals(&vertices, &indices);
    }

    #[test]
    fn light_helpers_fit_the_gpu_buffer_and_mark_each_source() {
        let lights = [Light {
            position: [2.0, 3.0, 4.0],
            color: [0.2, 0.5, 1.0],
            _pad_p: 0.0,
            _pad_c: 0.0,
        }; MAX_LIGHTS];
        let vertices = light_rays(&lights);
        assert_eq!(vertices.len(), MAX_LIGHTS * LIGHT_VERTICES_PER_LIGHT);
        for helper in vertices.as_chunks::<LIGHT_VERTICES_PER_LIGHT>().0 {
            assert_eq!(
                helper[LIGHT_VERTICES_PER_LIGHT - 2].position,
                lights[0].position
            );
            assert!(helper
                .iter()
                .all(|vertex| vertex.position.iter().all(|value| value.is_finite())));
        }
    }
    #[test]
    fn effects_uniform_layout_matches_wgsl() {
        assert_eq!(std::mem::size_of::<EffectsUniforms>(), 352);
        assert_eq!(
            std::mem::offset_of!(EffectsUniforms, reflection_matrix),
            256
        );
        assert_eq!(std::mem::offset_of!(EffectsUniforms, clip_plane), 320);
        assert_eq!(std::mem::offset_of!(EffectsUniforms, params), 336);
    }
    #[test]
    fn instance_material_layout_matches_shader() {
        use crate::visibility::InstanceData;
        assert_eq!(std::mem::size_of::<InstanceData>(), 144);
        assert_eq!(std::mem::offset_of!(InstanceData, material), 128);
        assert_eq!(
            InstanceData::layout()
                .attributes
                .last()
                .unwrap()
                .shader_location,
            11
        );
        assert_eq!(
            InstanceData::layout().attributes.last().unwrap().offset,
            128
        );
    }
    #[test]
    fn uniform_offsets_match_wgsl_alignment() {
        assert_eq!(std::mem::offset_of!(SceneUniforms, camera_pos), 64);
        assert_eq!(std::mem::offset_of!(SceneUniforms, lights), 80);
        assert_eq!(std::mem::size_of::<SceneUniforms>(), 208);
    }
}

impl crate::visibility::InstanceData {
    pub fn layout<'a>() -> VertexBufferLayout<'a> {
        const ATTRIBUTES: [wgpu::VertexAttribute; 9] = wgpu::vertex_attr_array![
            3 => Float32x4, 4 => Float32x4, 5 => Float32x4, 6 => Float32x4,
            7 => Float32x4, 8 => Float32x4, 9 => Float32x4, 10 => Float32x4, 11 => Float32x4
        ];
        VertexBufferLayout {
            array_stride: std::mem::size_of::<Self>() as u64,
            step_mode: wgpu::VertexStepMode::Instance,
            attributes: &ATTRIBUTES,
        }
    }
}

#[repr(C)]
#[derive(Clone, Copy, PartialEq, bytemuck::Pod, bytemuck::Zeroable)]
pub struct EffectsUniforms {
    pub shadow_matrices: [[[f32; 4]; 4]; MAX_LIGHTS],
    pub reflection_matrix: [[f32; 4]; 4],
    pub clip_plane: [f32; 4],
    pub params: [f32; 4],
}
impl Default for EffectsUniforms {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            shadow_matrices: [glam::Mat4::IDENTITY.to_cols_array_2d(); MAX_LIGHTS],
            reflection_matrix: glam::Mat4::IDENTITY.to_cols_array_2d(),
            clip_plane: [0.0; 4],
            params: [0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.00005],
        }
    }
}
