//! Browser presentation adapter. Only this module knows about canvas/surfaces.
use super::{
    depth,
    target::{RenderTarget, Viewport},
    textures::ColorTarget,
};
use wasm_bindgen::JsValue;
use web_sys::HtmlCanvasElement;
const DEPTH_FORMAT: wgpu::TextureFormat = wgpu::TextureFormat::Depth32Float;

pub struct CanvasFrame {
    frame: wgpu::SurfaceTexture,
    view: wgpu::TextureView,
    reconfigure: bool,
}
struct TextureOutput {
    target: ColorTarget,
    bind_group: wgpu::BindGroup,
    pipeline: wgpu::RenderPipeline,
}
pub struct CanvasOutput {
    instance: wgpu::Instance,
    surface: wgpu::Surface<'static>,
    canvas: HtmlCanvasElement,
    config: wgpu::SurfaceConfiguration,
    depth_texture: wgpu::Texture,
    depth_view: wgpu::TextureView,
    pub offscreen: bool,
    texture_output: Option<TextureOutput>,
}
impl CanvasOutput {
    pub async fn new(
        canvas: &HtmlCanvasElement,
    ) -> Result<(Self, wgpu::Device, wgpu::Queue, String), JsValue> {
        let instance_desc = wgpu::InstanceDescriptor {
            backends: if canvas.get_attribute("data-backend").as_deref() == Some("webgl") {
                wgpu::Backends::GL
            } else {
                wgpu::Backends::all()
            },
            ..wgpu::InstanceDescriptor::new_without_display_handle()
        };
        let instance = wgpu::util::new_instance_with_webgpu_detection(instance_desc).await;
        let surface = instance
            .create_surface(wgpu::SurfaceTarget::Canvas(canvas.clone()))
            .map_err(|e| JsValue::from_str(&format!("{e:?}")))?;
        let adapter = wgpu::util::initialize_adapter_from_env_or_default(&instance, Some(&surface))
            .await
            .map_err(|e| JsValue::from_str(&e.to_string()))?;
        let backend = format!("{:?}", adapter.get_info().backend);
        let (device, queue) = adapter
            .request_device(&wgpu::DeviceDescriptor {
                label: None,
                required_features: wgpu::Features::empty(),
                required_limits: wgpu::Limits::downlevel_webgl2_defaults()
                    .using_resolution(adapter.limits()),
                memory_hints: wgpu::MemoryHints::default(),
                trace: wgpu::Trace::default(),
                ..Default::default()
            })
            .await
            .map_err(|e| JsValue::from_str(&e.to_string()))?;
        let caps = surface.get_capabilities(&adapter);
        let format = caps
            .formats
            .iter()
            .copied()
            .find(wgpu::TextureFormat::is_srgb)
            .unwrap_or(caps.formats[0]);
        let view_format = format.add_srgb_suffix();
        let config = wgpu::SurfaceConfiguration {
            usage: wgpu::TextureUsages::RENDER_ATTACHMENT,
            format,
            width: canvas
                .width()
                .clamp(1, device.limits().max_texture_dimension_2d),
            height: canvas
                .height()
                .clamp(1, device.limits().max_texture_dimension_2d),
            present_mode: caps.present_modes[0],
            desired_maximum_frame_latency: 2,
            alpha_mode: caps.alpha_modes[0],
            view_formats: vec![view_format],
            color_space: wgpu::SurfaceColorSpace::Auto,
        };
        canvas.set_width(config.width);
        canvas.set_height(config.height);
        surface.configure(&device, &config);

        let depth_format = wgpu::TextureFormat::Depth32Float;
        let (depth_texture, depth_view) =
            depth::create(&device, config.width, config.height, depth_format);

        Ok((
            Self {
                instance,
                surface,
                canvas: canvas.clone(),
                config,
                depth_texture,
                depth_view,
                offscreen: false,
                texture_output: None,
            },
            device,
            queue,
            backend,
        ))
    }
    pub fn color_format(&self) -> wgpu::TextureFormat {
        self.config.format.add_srgb_suffix()
    }
    pub fn aspect(&self) -> f32 {
        self.config.width as f32 / self.config.height as f32
    }
    pub fn resize(&mut self, device: &wgpu::Device, width: u32, height: u32) {
        if width == 0 || height == 0 {
            return;
        }
        let limit = device.limits().max_texture_dimension_2d;
        let width = width.min(limit);
        let height = height.min(limit);
        if self.config.width == width && self.config.height == height {
            return;
        }
        self.canvas.set_width(width);
        self.canvas.set_height(height);
        self.config.width = width;
        self.config.height = height;
        self.surface.configure(device, &self.config);
        (self.depth_texture, self.depth_view) = depth::create(device, width, height, DEPTH_FORMAT);
        // Recreate attachments and their bind group together on the next frame.
        self.texture_output = None;
    }
    pub fn acquire(&mut self, device: &wgpu::Device) -> Result<Option<CanvasFrame>, JsValue> {
        let (frame, reconfigure) = match self.surface.get_current_texture() {
            wgpu::CurrentSurfaceTexture::Success(frame) => (frame, false),
            wgpu::CurrentSurfaceTexture::Suboptimal(frame) => (frame, true),
            wgpu::CurrentSurfaceTexture::Timeout | wgpu::CurrentSurfaceTexture::Occluded => {
                return Ok(None);
            }
            wgpu::CurrentSurfaceTexture::Outdated => {
                self.surface.configure(device, &self.config);
                return Ok(None);
            }
            wgpu::CurrentSurfaceTexture::Lost => {
                self.surface = self
                    .instance
                    .create_surface(wgpu::SurfaceTarget::Canvas(self.canvas.clone()))
                    .map_err(|error| JsValue::from_str(&error.to_string()))?;
                self.surface.configure(device, &self.config);
                return Ok(None);
            }
            wgpu::CurrentSurfaceTexture::Validation => {
                return Err(JsValue::from_str("GPU surface validation failed"));
            }
        };
        let view = frame.texture.create_view(&wgpu::TextureViewDescriptor {
            format: Some(self.config.format.add_srgb_suffix()),
            ..Default::default()
        });
        Ok(Some(CanvasFrame {
            frame,
            view,
            reconfigure,
        }))
    }
    pub fn ensure_target(&mut self, device: &wgpu::Device) {
        if !self.offscreen || self.texture_output.is_some() {
            return;
        }
        let format = self.config.format.add_srgb_suffix();
        let target = ColorTarget::new(
            device,
            "offscreen scene color",
            self.config.width,
            self.config.height,
            format,
        );
        let layout = device.create_bind_group_layout(&wgpu::BindGroupLayoutDescriptor {
            label: Some("present texture layout"),
            entries: &[
                wgpu::BindGroupLayoutEntry {
                    binding: 0,
                    visibility: wgpu::ShaderStages::FRAGMENT,
                    ty: wgpu::BindingType::Texture {
                        sample_type: wgpu::TextureSampleType::Float { filterable: true },
                        view_dimension: wgpu::TextureViewDimension::D2,
                        multisampled: false,
                    },
                    count: None,
                },
                wgpu::BindGroupLayoutEntry {
                    binding: 1,
                    visibility: wgpu::ShaderStages::FRAGMENT,
                    ty: wgpu::BindingType::Sampler(wgpu::SamplerBindingType::Filtering),
                    count: None,
                },
            ],
        });
        let sampler = device.create_sampler(&wgpu::SamplerDescriptor {
            label: Some("present sampler"),
            mag_filter: wgpu::FilterMode::Linear,
            min_filter: wgpu::FilterMode::Linear,
            ..Default::default()
        });
        let bind_group = device.create_bind_group(&wgpu::BindGroupDescriptor {
            label: Some("present texture"),
            layout: &layout,
            entries: &[
                wgpu::BindGroupEntry {
                    binding: 0,
                    resource: wgpu::BindingResource::TextureView(&target.color),
                },
                wgpu::BindGroupEntry {
                    binding: 1,
                    resource: wgpu::BindingResource::Sampler(&sampler),
                },
            ],
        });
        let shader = device.create_shader_module(wgpu::include_wgsl!("present.wgsl"));
        let pipeline_layout = device.create_pipeline_layout(&wgpu::PipelineLayoutDescriptor {
            label: Some("present layout"),
            bind_group_layouts: &[Some(&layout)],
            immediate_size: 0,
        });
        let pipeline = device.create_render_pipeline(&wgpu::RenderPipelineDescriptor {
            label: Some("present offscreen scene"),
            layout: Some(&pipeline_layout),
            vertex: wgpu::VertexState {
                module: &shader,
                entry_point: Some("vs_main"),
                compilation_options: Default::default(),
                buffers: &[],
            },
            fragment: Some(wgpu::FragmentState {
                module: &shader,
                entry_point: Some("fs_main"),
                compilation_options: Default::default(),
                targets: &[Some(wgpu::ColorTargetState {
                    format,
                    blend: Some(wgpu::BlendState::REPLACE),
                    write_mask: wgpu::ColorWrites::ALL,
                })],
            }),
            primitive: Default::default(),
            depth_stencil: None,
            multisample: Default::default(),
            multiview_mask: None,
            cache: None,
        });
        self.texture_output = Some(TextureOutput {
            target,
            bind_group,
            pipeline,
        });
    }
    pub fn target<'a>(&'a self, frame: &'a CanvasFrame) -> RenderTarget<'a> {
        let (color, depth) = if self.offscreen {
            let output = self
                .texture_output
                .as_ref()
                .expect("ensure_target before target");
            (&output.target.color, &output.target.depth)
        } else {
            (&frame.view, &self.depth_view)
        };
        RenderTarget {
            color,
            depth,
            auxiliary: None,
            color_format: self.config.format.add_srgb_suffix(),
            depth_format: DEPTH_FORMAT,
            width: self.config.width,
            height: self.config.height,
            viewport: Viewport::full(self.config.width, self.config.height),
            color_load: wgpu::LoadOp::Clear(wgpu::Color {
                r: 0.1,
                g: 0.1,
                b: 0.3,
                a: 1.0,
            }),
            depth_load: wgpu::LoadOp::Clear(1.0),
        }
    }
    pub fn present(&mut self, frame: CanvasFrame, device: &wgpu::Device, queue: &wgpu::Queue) {
        if self.offscreen {
            let output = self
                .texture_output
                .as_ref()
                .expect("ensure_target before present");
            let mut encoder = device.create_command_encoder(&wgpu::CommandEncoderDescriptor {
                label: Some("present encoder"),
            });
            {
                let mut pass = encoder.begin_render_pass(&wgpu::RenderPassDescriptor {
                    label: Some("present texture"),
                    color_attachments: &[Some(wgpu::RenderPassColorAttachment {
                        view: &frame.view,
                        depth_slice: None,
                        resolve_target: None,
                        ops: wgpu::Operations {
                            load: wgpu::LoadOp::Clear(wgpu::Color::BLACK),
                            store: wgpu::StoreOp::Store,
                        },
                    })],
                    depth_stencil_attachment: None,
                    timestamp_writes: None,
                    occlusion_query_set: None,
                    multiview_mask: None,
                });
                pass.set_pipeline(&output.pipeline);
                pass.set_bind_group(0, &output.bind_group, &[]);
                pass.draw(0..3, 0..1);
            }
            queue.submit(Some(encoder.finish()));
        }
        queue.present(frame.frame);
        if frame.reconfigure {
            self.surface.configure(device, &self.config);
        }
    }
}

\n# File: src/render/pipeline.rs
#![cfg(target_arch = "wasm32")]

use crate::render::data::Vertex;
use wgpu::{BindGroupLayout, Device, RenderPipeline, TextureFormat};

pub fn build(
    device: &Device,
    format: TextureFormat,
    depth_format: TextureFormat,
    layout: &BindGroupLayout,
    effects: &BindGroupLayout,
    reflected: bool,
    material_mask: bool,
) -> RenderPipeline {
    let shader = device.create_shader_module(wgpu::include_wgsl!("../shader.wgsl"));
    let pipeline_layout = device.create_pipeline_layout(&wgpu::PipelineLayoutDescriptor {
        label: Some("pipeline layout"),
        bind_group_layouts: &[Some(layout), Some(effects)],
        immediate_size: 0,
    });
    let mut targets = vec![Some(wgpu::ColorTargetState {
        format,
        blend: Some(wgpu::BlendState::REPLACE),
        write_mask: wgpu::ColorWrites::ALL,
    })];
    if material_mask {
        targets.push(Some(wgpu::ColorTargetState {
            format: TextureFormat::Rgba8Unorm,
            blend: Some(wgpu::BlendState::REPLACE),
            write_mask: wgpu::ColorWrites::ALL,
        }));
    }
    device.create_render_pipeline(&wgpu::RenderPipelineDescriptor {
        label: Some("pipeline"),
        layout: Some(&pipeline_layout),
        vertex: wgpu::VertexState {
            module: &shader,
            entry_point: Some("vs_main"),
            compilation_options: wgpu::PipelineCompilationOptions::default(),
            buffers: &[
                Some(Vertex::layout()),
                Some(crate::visibility::InstanceData::layout()),
            ],
        },
        fragment: Some(wgpu::FragmentState {
            module: &shader,
            entry_point: Some(if material_mask {
                "fs_postprocess"
            } else {
                "fs_main"
            }),
            compilation_options: wgpu::PipelineCompilationOptions::default(),
            targets: &targets,
        }),
        primitive: wgpu::PrimitiveState {
            topology: wgpu::PrimitiveTopology::TriangleList,
            // The left-handed camera projection reverses outward CCW meshes
            // in screen space. Cull CCW here and keep their visible CW faces.
            cull_mode: Some(if reflected {
                wgpu::Face::Back
            } else {
                wgpu::Face::Front
            }),
            front_face: wgpu::FrontFace::Ccw,
            ..Default::default()
        },
        depth_stencil: Some(wgpu::DepthStencilState {
            format: depth_format,
            depth_write_enabled: Some(true),
            depth_compare: Some(wgpu::CompareFunction::Less),
            stencil: Default::default(),
            bias: Default::default(),
        }),
        multisample: wgpu::MultisampleState::default(),
        multiview_mask: None,
        cache: None,
    })
}

pub fn build_lines(
    device: &Device,
    format: TextureFormat,
    depth_format: TextureFormat,
    layout: &BindGroupLayout,
) -> RenderPipeline {
    let shader = device.create_shader_module(wgpu::include_wgsl!("../shader.wgsl"));
    let pipeline_layout = device.create_pipeline_layout(&wgpu::PipelineLayoutDescriptor {
        label: Some("grid pipeline layout"),
        bind_group_layouts: &[Some(layout)],
        immediate_size: 0,
    });
    device.create_render_pipeline(&wgpu::RenderPipelineDescriptor {
        label: Some("grid pipeline"),
        layout: Some(&pipeline_layout),
        vertex: wgpu::VertexState {
            module: &shader,
            entry_point: Some("vs_lines"),
            compilation_options: wgpu::PipelineCompilationOptions::default(),
            buffers: &[Some(Vertex::layout())],
        },
        fragment: Some(wgpu::FragmentState {
            module: &shader,
            entry_point: Some("fs_color"),
            compilation_options: wgpu::PipelineCompilationOptions::default(),
            targets: &[Some(wgpu::ColorTargetState {
                format,
                blend: Some(wgpu::BlendState::REPLACE),
                write_mask: wgpu::ColorWrites::ALL,
            })],
        }),
        primitive: wgpu::PrimitiveState {
            topology: wgpu::PrimitiveTopology::LineList,
            cull_mode: None,
            front_face: wgpu::FrontFace::Ccw,
            ..Default::default()
        },
        depth_stencil: Some(wgpu::DepthStencilState {
            format: depth_format,
            depth_write_enabled: Some(false),
            depth_compare: Some(wgpu::CompareFunction::Less),
            stencil: Default::default(),
            bias: Default::default(),
        }),
        multisample: wgpu::MultisampleState::default(),
        multiview_mask: None,
        cache: None,
    })
}

pub fn build_shadow(
    device: &Device,
    layout: &BindGroupLayout,
    format: TextureFormat,
) -> RenderPipeline {
    let shader = device.create_shader_module(wgpu::include_wgsl!("shadow.wgsl"));
    let pipeline_layout = device.create_pipeline_layout(&wgpu::PipelineLayoutDescriptor {
        label: Some("shadow layout"),
        bind_group_layouts: &[Some(layout)],
        immediate_size: 0,
    });
    device.create_render_pipeline(&wgpu::RenderPipelineDescriptor {
        label: Some("depth-only shadows"),
        layout: Some(&pipeline_layout),
        vertex: wgpu::VertexState {
            module: &shader,
            entry_point: Some("vs_main"),
            compilation_options: Default::default(),
            buffers: &[
                Some(Vertex::layout()),
                Some(crate::visibility::InstanceData::layout()),
            ],
        },
        fragment: None,
        primitive: wgpu::PrimitiveState {
            cull_mode: None,
            ..Default::default()
        },
        depth_stencil: Some(wgpu::DepthStencilState {
            format,
            depth_write_enabled: Some(true),
            depth_compare: Some(wgpu::CompareFunction::Less),
            stencil: Default::default(),
            bias: wgpu::DepthBiasState {
                constant: 2,
                slope_scale: 2.0,
                clamp: 0.0,
            },
        }),
        multisample: Default::default(),
        multiview_mask: None,
        cache: None,
    })
}

\n# File: src/render/postprocess.rs
//! Depth-aware fragment postprocessing, independent of a browser or surface.
use super::{
    data::as_bytes,
    target::{RenderTarget, RenderView},
    textures::ColorTarget,
};
use glam::{Vec2, Vec3};

#[repr(C)]
#[derive(Clone, Copy, bytemuck::Pod, bytemuck::Zeroable)]
struct Uniforms {
    inverse_view_projection: [[f32; 4]; 4],
    view_projection: [[f32; 4]; 4],
    camera: [f32; 4],
    light_position: [f32; 4],
    light_color: [f32; 4],
    // strength, final output flag, width, height; all fields are 16-byte aligned.
    params: [f32; 4],
    mask_params: [f32; 4],
    shadow_view_projection: [[f32; 4]; 4],
    volume_params: [f32; 4],
}
// Rust offsets match WGSL matrices and vec4 fields (288 bytes total).
const _: () = assert!(std::mem::size_of::<Uniforms>() == 288);
const _: () = assert!(std::mem::offset_of!(Uniforms, params) == 176);

// Add a WGSL fragment entry point and a descriptor to extend the chain.
// All passes read original scene depth; intermediate alpha is material metadata.
struct EffectDescriptor {
    name: &'static str,
    entry_point: &'static str,
    shader_source: &'static str,
}
const EFFECTS: &[EffectDescriptor] = &[
    EffectDescriptor {
        name: "ssr",
        entry_point: "fs_ssr",
        shader_source: include_str!("postprocess.wgsl"),
    },
    EffectDescriptor {
        name: "godRays",
        entry_point: "fs_god_rays",
        shader_source: include_str!("postprocess.wgsl"),
    },
];
struct Effect {
    name: &'static str,
    enabled: bool,
    strength: f32,
    pipeline: wgpu::RenderPipeline,
    uniform: wgpu::Buffer,
}
struct Intermediate {
    _texture: wgpu::Texture,
    view: wgpu::TextureView,
}
impl Intermediate {
    fn new(device: &wgpu::Device, width: u32, height: u32, format: wgpu::TextureFormat) -> Self {
        let texture = device.create_texture(&wgpu::TextureDescriptor {
            label: Some("postprocess ping-pong"),
            size: wgpu::Extent3d {
                width,
                height,
                depth_or_array_layers: 1,
            },
            mip_level_count: 1,
            sample_count: 1,
            dimension: wgpu::TextureDimension::D2,
            format,
            usage: wgpu::TextureUsages::RENDER_ATTACHMENT | wgpu::TextureUsages::TEXTURE_BINDING,
            view_formats: &[],
        });
        let view = texture.create_view(&Default::default());
        Self {
            _texture: texture,
            view,
        }
    }
}
struct ReflectionMask {
    texture: wgpu::Texture,
    view: wgpu::TextureView,
    params: [f32; 4],
    enabled: bool,
    needs_white_upload: bool,
}
impl ReflectionMask {
    fn new(device: &wgpu::Device, width: u32, height: u32) -> Self {
        let texture = device.create_texture(&wgpu::TextureDescriptor {
            label: Some("world XZ reflection mask"),
            size: wgpu::Extent3d {
                width,
                height,
                depth_or_array_layers: 1,
            },
            mip_level_count: 1,
            sample_count: 1,
            dimension: wgpu::TextureDimension::D2,
            format: wgpu::TextureFormat::R8Unorm,
            usage: wgpu::TextureUsages::TEXTURE_BINDING | wgpu::TextureUsages::COPY_DST,
            view_formats: &[],
        });
        let view = texture.create_view(&Default::default());
        Self {
            texture,
            view,
            params: [0.0, 0.0, 1.0, 1.0],
            enabled: false,
            needs_white_upload: true,
        }
    }
    fn upload(&self, queue: &wgpu::Queue, width: u32, height: u32, pixels: &[u8]) {
        queue.write_texture(
            wgpu::TexelCopyTextureInfo {
                texture: &self.texture,
                mip_level: 0,
                origin: wgpu::Origin3d::ZERO,
                aspect: wgpu::TextureAspect::All,
            },
            pixels,
            wgpu::TexelCopyBufferLayout {
                offset: 0,
                bytes_per_row: Some(width),
                rows_per_image: Some(height),
            },
            wgpu::Extent3d {
                width,
                height,
                depth_or_array_layers: 1,
            },
        );
    }
}
struct Targets {
    scene: ColorTarget,
    depth_color: Intermediate,
    ping: [Intermediate; 2],
    bind_groups: Vec<[wgpu::BindGroup; 3]>,
}
pub struct PostProcessor {
    format: wgpu::TextureFormat,
    layout: wgpu::BindGroupLayout,
    sampler: wgpu::Sampler,
    depth_sampler: wgpu::Sampler,
    shadow_layout: wgpu::BindGroupLayout,
    shadow_sampler: wgpu::Sampler,
    shadow_bind_group: wgpu::BindGroup,
    shadow_views: Option<(wgpu::TextureView, wgpu::TextureView)>,
    effects: Vec<Effect>,
    reflection_mask: ReflectionMask,
    targets: Option<Targets>,
    submitted_frames: u64,
    last_pass_count: usize,
    volume_density: f32,
    volume_exposure: f32,
}
impl PostProcessor {
    pub fn new(device: &wgpu::Device, format: wgpu::TextureFormat) -> Self {
        let layout = device.create_bind_group_layout(&wgpu::BindGroupLayoutDescriptor {
            label: Some("postprocess layout"),
            entries: &[
                wgpu::BindGroupLayoutEntry {
                    binding: 0,
                    visibility: wgpu::ShaderStages::FRAGMENT,
                    ty: wgpu::BindingType::Texture {
                        sample_type: wgpu::TextureSampleType::Float { filterable: true },
                        view_dimension: wgpu::TextureViewDimension::D2,
                        multisampled: false,
                    },
                    count: None,
                },
                wgpu::BindGroupLayoutEntry {
                    binding: 1,
                    visibility: wgpu::ShaderStages::FRAGMENT,
                    ty: wgpu::BindingType::Sampler(wgpu::SamplerBindingType::Filtering),
                    count: None,
                },
                wgpu::BindGroupLayoutEntry {
                    binding: 2,
                    visibility: wgpu::ShaderStages::FRAGMENT,
                    ty: wgpu::BindingType::Texture {
                        sample_type: wgpu::TextureSampleType::Float { filterable: true },
                        view_dimension: wgpu::TextureViewDimension::D2,
                        multisampled: false,
                    },
                    count: None,
                },
                wgpu::BindGroupLayoutEntry {
                    binding: 3,
                    visibility: wgpu::ShaderStages::FRAGMENT,
                    ty: wgpu::BindingType::Buffer {
                        ty: wgpu::BufferBindingType::Uniform,
                        has_dynamic_offset: false,
                        min_binding_size: wgpu::BufferSize::new(
                            std::mem::size_of::<Uniforms>() as u64
                        ),
                    },
                    count: None,
                },
                wgpu::BindGroupLayoutEntry {
                    binding: 4,
                    visibility: wgpu::ShaderStages::FRAGMENT,
                    ty: wgpu::BindingType::Sampler(wgpu::SamplerBindingType::NonFiltering),
                    count: None,
                },
                wgpu::BindGroupLayoutEntry {
                    binding: 6,
                    visibility: wgpu::ShaderStages::FRAGMENT,
                    ty: wgpu::BindingType::Texture {
                        sample_type: wgpu::TextureSampleType::Float { filterable: true },
                        view_dimension: wgpu::TextureViewDimension::D2,
                        multisampled: false,
                    },
                    count: None,
                },
            ],
        });
        let sampler = device.create_sampler(&wgpu::SamplerDescriptor {
            label: Some("postprocess linear clamp"),
            mag_filter: wgpu::FilterMode::Linear,
            min_filter: wgpu::FilterMode::Linear,
            ..Default::default()
        });
        let depth_sampler = device.create_sampler(&wgpu::SamplerDescriptor {
            label: Some("postprocess nearest depth"),
            ..Default::default()
        });
        let shadow_layout = device.create_bind_group_layout(&wgpu::BindGroupLayoutDescriptor {
            label: Some("volume shadow layout"),
            entries: &[
                wgpu::BindGroupLayoutEntry {
                    binding: 0,
                    visibility: wgpu::ShaderStages::FRAGMENT,
                    ty: wgpu::BindingType::Texture {
                        sample_type: wgpu::TextureSampleType::Depth,
                        view_dimension: wgpu::TextureViewDimension::D2,
                        multisampled: false,
                    },
                    count: None,
                },
                wgpu::BindGroupLayoutEntry {
                    binding: 1,
                    visibility: wgpu::ShaderStages::FRAGMENT,
                    ty: wgpu::BindingType::Texture {
                        sample_type: wgpu::TextureSampleType::Depth,
                        view_dimension: wgpu::TextureViewDimension::D2,
                        multisampled: false,
                    },
                    count: None,
                },
                wgpu::BindGroupLayoutEntry {
                    binding: 2,
                    visibility: wgpu::ShaderStages::FRAGMENT,
                    ty: wgpu::BindingType::Sampler(wgpu::SamplerBindingType::Comparison),
                    count: None,
                },
            ],
        });
        let shadow_sampler = device.create_sampler(&wgpu::SamplerDescriptor {
            label: Some("volume comparison sampler"),
            compare: Some(wgpu::CompareFunction::LessEqual),
            mag_filter: wgpu::FilterMode::Linear,
            min_filter: wgpu::FilterMode::Linear,
            ..Default::default()
        });
        // Valid fallback resources until the renderer supplies its persistent maps.
        // Their contents are not sampled when shadows_enabled is false.
        let fallback = super::textures::DepthTarget::new(device, "volume shadow fallback", 1);
        let shadow_bind_group = Self::shadow_group(
            device,
            &shadow_layout,
            &shadow_sampler,
            &fallback.view,
            &fallback.view,
        );
        let effects = EFFECTS
            .iter()
            .map(|descriptor| {
                let shader = device.create_shader_module(wgpu::ShaderModuleDescriptor {
                    label: Some(descriptor.name),
                    source: wgpu::ShaderSource::Wgsl(descriptor.shader_source.into()),
                });
                let pipeline_layout =
                    device.create_pipeline_layout(&wgpu::PipelineLayoutDescriptor {
                        label: Some("postprocess pipeline layout"),
                        bind_group_layouts: &[Some(&layout), Some(&shadow_layout)],
                        immediate_size: 0,
                    });
                let pipeline = device.create_render_pipeline(&wgpu::RenderPipelineDescriptor {
                    label: Some("depth-aware postprocess"),
                    layout: Some(&pipeline_layout),
                    vertex: wgpu::VertexState {
                        module: &shader,
                        entry_point: Some("vs_main"),
                        compilation_options: Default::default(),
                        buffers: &[],
                    },
                    fragment: Some(wgpu::FragmentState {
                        module: &shader,
                        entry_point: Some(descriptor.entry_point),
                        compilation_options: Default::default(),
                        targets: &[Some(wgpu::ColorTargetState {
                            format,
                            blend: Some(wgpu::BlendState::REPLACE),
                            write_mask: wgpu::ColorWrites::ALL,
                        })],
                    }),
                    primitive: Default::default(),
                    depth_stencil: None,
                    multisample: Default::default(),
                    multiview_mask: None,
                    cache: None,
                });
                Effect {
                    name: descriptor.name,
                    pipeline,
                    enabled: false,
                    strength: 0.5,
                    uniform: device.create_buffer(&wgpu::BufferDescriptor {
                        label: Some(descriptor.name),
                        size: std::mem::size_of::<Uniforms>() as u64,
                        usage: wgpu::BufferUsages::UNIFORM | wgpu::BufferUsages::COPY_DST,
                        mapped_at_creation: false,
                    }),
                }
            })
            .collect();
        Self {
            format,
            layout,
            sampler,
            depth_sampler,
            shadow_layout,
            shadow_sampler,
            shadow_bind_group,
            shadow_views: None,
            effects,
            reflection_mask: ReflectionMask::new(device, 1, 1),
            targets: None,
            submitted_frames: 0,
            last_pass_count: 0,
            volume_density: 0.035,
            volume_exposure: 2.5,
        }
    }
    fn shadow_group(
        device: &wgpu::Device,
        layout: &wgpu::BindGroupLayout,
        sampler: &wgpu::Sampler,
        static_map: &wgpu::TextureView,
        dynamic_map: &wgpu::TextureView,
    ) -> wgpu::BindGroup {
        device.create_bind_group(&wgpu::BindGroupDescriptor {
            label: Some("volume first-light shadows"),
            layout,
            entries: &[
                wgpu::BindGroupEntry {
                    binding: 0,
                    resource: wgpu::BindingResource::TextureView(static_map),
                },
                wgpu::BindGroupEntry {
                    binding: 1,
                    resource: wgpu::BindingResource::TextureView(dynamic_map),
                },
                wgpu::BindGroupEntry {
                    binding: 2,
                    resource: wgpu::BindingResource::Sampler(sampler),
                },
            ],
        })
    }
    /// Rebind only when the renderer replaces its first-light shadow textures.
    pub fn set_shadow_maps(
        &mut self,
        device: &wgpu::Device,
        static_map: &wgpu::TextureView,
        dynamic_map: &wgpu::TextureView,
    ) {
        if self
            .shadow_views
            .as_ref()
            .is_some_and(|(cached_static, cached_dynamic)| {
                cached_static == static_map && cached_dynamic == dynamic_map
            })
        {
            return;
        }
        self.shadow_bind_group = Self::shadow_group(
            device,
            &self.shadow_layout,
            &self.shadow_sampler,
            static_map,
            dynamic_map,
        );
        self.shadow_views = Some((static_map.clone(), dynamic_map.clone()));
    }
    /// R8 bytes map world XZ to UV: (position - center) / size + 0.5.
    /// Wet/dry base-color shading is applied by the SSR pass only.
    #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)] // Explicit GPU/FFI parameter layout.
    pub fn set_reflection_mask(
        &mut self,
        device: &wgpu::Device,
        queue: &wgpu::Queue,
        width: u32,
        height: u32,
        pixels: &[u8],
        center: Vec2,
        size: Vec2,
    ) -> bool {
        if width == 0
            || height == 0
            || width > 1024
            || height > 1024
            || !center.is_finite()
            || !size.is_finite()
            || size.x <= 0.0
            || size.y <= 0.0
            || width
                .checked_mul(height)
                .and_then(|n| usize::try_from(n).ok())
                != Some(pixels.len())
        {
            return false;
        }
        let mut mask = ReflectionMask::new(device, width, height);
        mask.upload(queue, width, height, pixels);
        mask.params = [center.x, center.y, size.x, size.y];
        mask.enabled = true;
        mask.needs_white_upload = false;
        self.reflection_mask = mask;
        self.targets = None;
        self.last_pass_count = 0;
        true
    }
    pub fn configure_volume(&mut self, density: f32, exposure: f32) -> bool {
        if !density.is_finite()
            || !(0.0..=1.0).contains(&density)
            || !exposure.is_finite()
            || !(0.0..=100.0).contains(&exposure)
        {
            return false;
        }
        self.volume_density = density;
        self.volume_exposure = exposure;
        true
    }

    pub fn set_effect(&mut self, name: &str, enabled: bool, strength: f32) -> bool {
        if !strength.is_finite() || !(0.0..=1.0).contains(&strength) {
            return false;
        }
        let Some(effect) = self.effects.iter_mut().find(|effect| effect.name == name) else {
            return false;
        };
        effect.enabled = enabled;
        effect.strength = strength;
        if !self.enabled() {
            self.targets = None;
            self.last_pass_count = 0;
        }
        true
    }
    pub fn enabled(&self) -> bool {
        self.effects
            .iter()
            .any(|effect| effect.enabled && effect.strength > 0.0)
    }
    pub fn ensure_size(&mut self, device: &wgpu::Device, width: u32, height: u32) {
        if !self.enabled() || width == 0 || height == 0 {
            self.targets = None;
            self.last_pass_count = 0;
            return;
        }
        if self
            .targets
            .as_ref()
            .is_some_and(|targets| targets.scene.width == width && targets.scene.height == height)
        {
            return;
        }
        let scene = ColorTarget::new(device, "postprocess scene", width, height, self.format);
        let depth_color = Intermediate::new(device, width, height, wgpu::TextureFormat::Rgba8Unorm);
        let ping: [Intermediate; 2] =
            std::array::from_fn(|_| Intermediate::new(device, width, height, self.format));
        let sources = [&scene.color, &ping[0].view, &ping[1].view];
        let bind_groups = self.effects.iter().map(|effect| {
            std::array::from_fn(|source_index| {
                device.create_bind_group(&wgpu::BindGroupDescriptor {
                    label: Some(effect.name),
                    layout: &self.layout,
                    entries: &[
                        wgpu::BindGroupEntry {
                            binding: 0,
                            resource: wgpu::BindingResource::TextureView(sources[source_index]),
                        },
                        wgpu::BindGroupEntry {
                            binding: 1,
                            resource: wgpu::BindingResource::Sampler(&self.sampler),
                        },
                        wgpu::BindGroupEntry {
                            binding: 2,
                            resource: wgpu::BindingResource::TextureView(&depth_color.view),
                        },
                        wgpu::BindGroupEntry {
                            binding: 3,
                            resource: effect.uniform.as_entire_binding(),
                        },
                        wgpu::BindGroupEntry {
                            binding: 4,
                            resource: wgpu::BindingResource::Sampler(&self.depth_sampler),
                        },
                        wgpu::BindGroupEntry {
                            binding: 6,
                            resource: wgpu::BindingResource::TextureView(
                                &self.reflection_mask.view,
                            ),
                        },
                    ],
                })
            })
        });
        let bind_groups = bind_groups.collect();
        self.targets = Some(Targets {
            scene,
            depth_color,
            ping,
            bind_groups,
        });
    }
    /// Call ensure_size before requesting the scene render attachments.
    pub fn target(&self) -> RenderTarget<'_> {
        let targets = self
            .targets
            .as_ref()
            .expect("ensure_size before postprocess target");
        let mut target = targets.scene.target();
        target.auxiliary = Some(&targets.depth_color.view);
        target
    }
    /// Frames count successful queue.submit calls, not GPU completion timestamps.
    pub fn stats(&self) -> [f64; 5] {
        let passes = if self.enabled() {
            self.last_pass_count as f64
        } else {
            0.0
        };
        self.targets.as_ref().map_or(
            [passes, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0, self.submitted_frames as f64],
            |targets| {
                [
                    passes,
                    targets.scene.width as f64,
                    targets.scene.height as f64,
                    4.0,
                    self.submitted_frames as f64,
                ]
            },
        )
    }
    #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)] // Explicit GPU/FFI parameter layout.
    pub fn render(
        &mut self,
        device: &wgpu::Device,
        queue: &wgpu::Queue,
        view: RenderView,
        light_position: Vec3,
        light_color: Vec3,
        output: &wgpu::TextureView,
        shadows_enabled: bool,
    ) {
        // new() has no queue; initialize its white fallback once, before sampling.
        if self.reflection_mask.needs_white_upload {
            self.reflection_mask.upload(queue, 1, 1, &[255]);
            self.reflection_mask.needs_white_upload = false;
        }
        let Some(targets) = &self.targets else {
            return;
        };
        let active: Vec<_> = self
            .effects
            .iter()
            .enumerate()
            .filter(|(_, effect)| effect.enabled && effect.strength > 0.0)
            .collect();
        if active.is_empty() {
            return;
        }
        let shadow_matrix = crate::render_math::shadow_view_projection(light_position);
        let mut encoder = device.create_command_encoder(&wgpu::CommandEncoderDescriptor {
            label: Some("postprocess chain"),
        });
        for (index, (effect_index, effect)) in active.iter().enumerate() {
            let uniforms = Uniforms {
                inverse_view_projection: view.view_projection.inverse().to_cols_array_2d(),
                view_projection: view.view_projection.to_cols_array_2d(),
                camera: view
                    .camera_position
                    .extend(if self.reflection_mask.enabled {
                        1.0
                    } else {
                        0.0
                    })
                    .to_array(),
                light_position: light_position.extend(1.0).to_array(),
                light_color: light_color.extend(1.0).to_array(),
                params: [
                    effect.strength,
                    if index + 1 == active.len() { 1.0 } else { 0.0 },
                    targets.scene.width as f32,
                    targets.scene.height as f32,
                ],
                mask_params: self.reflection_mask.params,
                shadow_view_projection: shadow_matrix.to_cols_array_2d(),
                volume_params: [
                    if shadows_enabled { 1.0 } else { 0.0 },
                    self.volume_density,
                    24.0,
                    self.volume_exposure,
                ],
            };
            queue.write_buffer(&effect.uniform, 0, as_bytes(&[uniforms]));
            let destination = if index + 1 == active.len() {
                output
            } else {
                &targets.ping[index % 2].view
            };
            let source_index = if index == 0 { 0 } else { 1 + (index - 1) % 2 };
            let bind_group = &targets.bind_groups[*effect_index][source_index];
            let mut pass = encoder.begin_render_pass(&wgpu::RenderPassDescriptor {
                label: Some(effect.name),
                color_attachments: &[Some(wgpu::RenderPassColorAttachment {
                    view: destination,
                    depth_slice: None,
                    resolve_target: None,
                    ops: wgpu::Operations {
                        load: wgpu::LoadOp::Clear(wgpu::Color::BLACK),
                        store: wgpu::StoreOp::Store,
                    },
                })],
                depth_stencil_attachment: None,
                timestamp_writes: None,
                occlusion_query_set: None,
                multiview_mask: None,
            });
            pass.set_pipeline(&effect.pipeline);
            pass.set_bind_group(0, bind_group, &[]);
            pass.set_bind_group(1, &self.shadow_bind_group, &[]);
            pass.draw(0..3, 0..1);
        }
        queue.submit(Some(encoder.finish()));
        self.last_pass_count = active.len();
        self.submitted_frames = self.submitted_frames.saturating_add(1);
    }
}

\n# File: src/render/postprocess.wgsl
struct Uniforms {
    inverse_view_projection: mat4x4<f32>,
    view_projection: mat4x4<f32>,
    camera: vec4<f32>,
    light_position: vec4<f32>,
    light_color: vec4<f32>,
    params: vec4<f32>,
    mask_params: vec4<f32>,
    shadow_view_projection: mat4x4<f32>,
    // shadow-enabled, extinction density, max ray range, scattering exposure
    volume_params: vec4<f32>,
};
@group(0) @binding(0) var scene_texture: texture_2d<f32>;
@group(0) @binding(1) var scene_sampler: sampler;
@group(0) @binding(2) var scene_depth: texture_2d<f32>;
@group(0) @binding(3) var<uniform> uniforms: Uniforms;
@group(0) @binding(4) var depth_sampler: sampler;
@group(0) @binding(6) var reflection_mask: texture_2d<f32>;
@group(1) @binding(0) var volume_static_shadow: texture_depth_2d;
@group(1) @binding(1) var volume_dynamic_shadow: texture_depth_2d;
@group(1) @binding(2) var volume_shadow_sampler: sampler_comparison;
struct Output { @builtin(position) position: vec4<f32>, @location(0) uv: vec2<f32> };
@vertex fn vs_main(@builtin(vertex_index) index: u32) -> Output {
    let positions = array<vec2<f32>, 3>(vec2(-1.0, -1.0), vec2(3.0, -1.0), vec2(-1.0, 3.0));
    let p = positions[index];
    var output: Output;
    output.position = vec4(p, 0.0, 1.0);
    output.uv = vec2((p.x + 1.0) * 0.5, (1.0 - p.y) * 0.5);
    return output;
}
fn depth_at(uv: vec2<f32>) -> f32 {
    let size = vec2<i32>(uniforms.params.zw);
    let pixel = clamp(vec2<i32>(uv * uniforms.params.zw), vec2<i32>(0), size - vec2<i32>(1));
    // RGB base-255 depth packing avoids depth-sampler LOD limitations in GLSL ES300.
    // An auxiliary attachment cleared to white represents untouched far depth.
    let packed = textureSampleLevel(scene_depth, depth_sampler, (vec2<f32>(pixel) + vec2(0.5)) / uniforms.params.zw, 0.0).rgb;
    return clamp(dot(packed, vec3(1.0, 1.0 / 255.0, 1.0 / 65025.0)), 0.0, 1.0);
}
fn world_at(uv: vec2<f32>, depth: f32) -> vec3<f32> {
    let p = uniforms.inverse_view_projection * vec4(uv.x * 2.0 - 1.0, 1.0 - uv.y * 2.0, depth, 1.0);
    return p.xyz / p.w;
}
fn inside(uv: vec2<f32>) -> bool { return all(uv > vec2(0.0)) && all(uv < vec2(1.0)); }
fn scene_at(uv: vec2<f32>) -> vec4<f32> { return textureSampleLevel(scene_texture, scene_sampler, uv, 0.0); }
fn reflection_mask_at(position: vec3<f32>) -> f32 {
    if uniforms.camera.w < 0.5 { return 1.0; }
    let mask_uv = (position.xz - uniforms.mask_params.xy) / uniforms.mask_params.zw + vec2(0.5);
    if !all(mask_uv >= vec2(0.0)) || !all(mask_uv <= vec2(1.0)) { return 0.0; }
    return textureSampleLevel(reflection_mask, scene_sampler, mask_uv, 0.0).r;
}
fn reflected_color(uv: vec2<f32>, source: vec4<f32>) -> vec3<f32> {
    let depth = depth_at(uv);
    if depth >= 0.999999 || source.a <= 0.001 { return source.rgb; }
    let position = world_at(uv, depth);
    // Choose the nearer depth neighbour on each axis to reduce silhouette normals.
    let pixel = 1.0 / uniforms.params.zw;
    let left = world_at(uv - vec2(pixel.x, 0.0), depth_at(uv - vec2(pixel.x, 0.0)));
    let right = world_at(uv + vec2(pixel.x, 0.0), depth_at(uv + vec2(pixel.x, 0.0)));
    let up = world_at(uv - vec2(0.0, pixel.y), depth_at(uv - vec2(0.0, pixel.y)));
    let down = world_at(uv + vec2(0.0, pixel.y), depth_at(uv + vec2(0.0, pixel.y)));
    let dx = select(position - left, right - position, distance(right, position) < distance(left, position));
    let dy = select(position - up, down - position, distance(down, position) < distance(up, position));
    let raw_normal = cross(dx, dy);
    if dot(raw_normal, raw_normal) < 0.000000000001 { return source.rgb; }
    let incident = normalize(position - uniforms.camera.xyz);
    var normal = normalize(raw_normal);
    if dot(normal, incident) > 0.0 { normal = -normal; }
    let direction = reflect(incident, normal);
    let camera_distance = distance(position, uniforms.camera.xyz);
    let step_length = clamp(camera_distance * 0.025, 0.04, 0.5);
    let thickness = clamp(camera_distance * 0.012, 0.06, 0.4);
    let origin = position + normal * max(0.025, thickness * 0.5);
    var previous_t = 0.0;
    var previous_delta = -thickness;
    for (var step = 1u; step <= 64u; step += 1u) {
        let t = f32(step) * step_length;
        let ray = origin + direction * t;
        let clip = uniforms.view_projection * vec4(ray, 1.0);
        if clip.w <= 0.0 { break; }
        let ndc = clip.xyz / clip.w;
        let sample_uv = vec2(ndc.x * 0.5 + 0.5, 0.5 - ndc.y * 0.5);
        if !inside(sample_uv) || ndc.z < 0.0 || ndc.z > 1.0 { break; }
        let sampled_depth = depth_at(sample_uv);
        if sampled_depth >= 0.999999 { previous_t = t; previous_delta = -thickness; continue; }
        let surface = world_at(sample_uv, sampled_depth);
        // Signed separation along the camera ray detects front-to-back crossings.
        let eye_direction = normalize(ray - uniforms.camera.xyz);
        let delta = dot(ray - surface, eye_direction);
        if step > 2u && previous_delta < 0.0 && delta >= 0.0 {
            var low = previous_t;
            var high = t;
            var hit_uv = sample_uv;
            var hit_delta = delta;
            // Refine the crossing; reject depth discontinuities thicker than geometry budget.
            for (var refine = 0u; refine < 5u; refine += 1u) {
                let mid = (low + high) * 0.5;
                let mid_ray = origin + direction * mid;
                let mid_clip = uniforms.view_projection * vec4(mid_ray, 1.0);
                let mid_ndc = mid_clip.xyz / mid_clip.w;
                let mid_uv = vec2(mid_ndc.x * 0.5 + 0.5, 0.5 - mid_ndc.y * 0.5);
                let mid_surface = world_at(mid_uv, depth_at(mid_uv));
                let mid_delta = dot(mid_ray - mid_surface, normalize(mid_ray - uniforms.camera.xyz));
                if mid_delta >= 0.0 { high = mid; hit_uv = mid_uv; hit_delta = mid_delta; } else { low = mid; }
            }
            let separation = length((hit_uv - uv) * uniforms.params.zw);
            if hit_delta < thickness && separation > 3.0 {
                let border = min(min(hit_uv.x, 1.0 - hit_uv.x), min(hit_uv.y, 1.0 - hit_uv.y));
                let edge_fade = smoothstep(0.0, 0.08, border);
                let range_fade = 1.0 - smoothstep(40.0, 64.0, f32(step));
                let fresnel = 0.2 + 0.8 * pow(1.0 - max(dot(-incident, normal), 0.0), 5.0);
                let weight = clamp(source.a * reflection_mask_at(position) * uniforms.params.x * edge_fade * range_fade * fresnel, 0.0, 1.0);
                return mix(source.rgb, scene_at(hit_uv).rgb, weight);
            }
        }
        previous_t = t;
        previous_delta = delta;
    }
    return source.rgb;
}
// Single-scattering eye-ray integration with shadow-map visibility, inspired by
// Toth/Umenhoffer (Eurographics 2009). No radial screen emitter or screen-light gating.
fn volume_visibility(position: vec3<f32>) -> f32 {
    let clip = uniforms.shadow_view_projection * vec4(position, 1.0);
    if clip.w <= 0.0 { return 0.0; }
    let ndc = clip.xyz / clip.w;
    let uv = vec2(ndc.x * 0.5 + 0.5, 0.5 - ndc.y * 0.5);
    // Existing point-light shadow maps cover a perspective cone, not all 360 degrees.
    if !inside(uv) || ndc.z <= 0.0 || ndc.z >= 1.0 { return 0.0; }
    let edge = min(min(uv.x, 1.0 - uv.x), min(uv.y, 1.0 - uv.y));
    let cone_fade = smoothstep(0.0, 0.04, edge);
    // With shadows disabled, retain explicitly unshadowed scattering in this cone.
    if uniforms.volume_params.x < 0.5 { return cone_fade; }
    // A small depth bias avoids self-shadowing without leaking light through stones.
    let reference_depth = ndc.z - 0.00008;
    let static_visibility = textureSampleCompareLevel(volume_static_shadow, volume_shadow_sampler, uv, reference_depth);
    let dynamic_visibility = textureSampleCompareLevel(volume_dynamic_shadow, volume_shadow_sampler, uv, reference_depth);
    return min(static_visibility, dynamic_visibility) * cone_fade;
}
fn god_rays(uv: vec2<f32>, source: vec3<f32>) -> vec3<f32> {
    if max(max(uniforms.light_color.r, uniforms.light_color.g), uniforms.light_color.b) <= 0.0 { return source; }
    let depth = depth_at(uv);
    let endpoint = world_at(uv, min(depth, 0.999999));
    let camera_to_surface = endpoint - uniforms.camera.xyz;
    let surface_distance = length(camera_to_surface);
    if surface_distance < 0.0001 { return source; }
    let direction = camera_to_surface / surface_distance;
    var ray_length = min(surface_distance, uniforms.volume_params.z);
    if depth >= 0.999999 { ray_length = uniforms.volume_params.z; }
    let step_length = ray_length / 32.0;
    let density = uniforms.volume_params.y * uniforms.params.x;
    let step_transmission = exp(-density * step_length);
    // Deterministic per-pixel jitter removes aligned bands without temporal flicker.
    let pixel = floor(uv * uniforms.params.zw);
    let jitter = fract(52.9829189 * fract(dot(pixel, vec2(0.06711056, 0.00583715))));
    var transmission = 1.0;
    var radiance = vec3(0.0);
    let g = 0.45;
    for (var step = 0u; step < 32u; step += 1u) {
        let t = (f32(step) + jitter) * step_length;
        let position = uniforms.camera.xyz + direction * t;
        let to_light = uniforms.light_position.xyz - position;
        let distance_squared = max(dot(to_light, to_light), 0.01);
        let light_direction = to_light * inverseSqrt(distance_squared);
        let cosine = clamp(dot(direction, light_direction), -1.0, 1.0);
        // Henyey-Greenstein phase with moderate forward scattering.
        let phase = (1.0 - g * g) / (12.5663706 * pow(max(1.0 + g * g - 2.0 * g * cosine, 0.001), 1.5));
        let attenuation = 5.0 / (1.0 + 0.2 * distance_squared);
        let visibility = volume_visibility(position);
        radiance += max(uniforms.light_color.rgb, vec3(0.0)) * (transmission * (1.0 - step_transmission) * phase * attenuation * visibility);
        transmission *= step_transmission;
    }
    // Keep original surface lighting; the integrated medium adds bounded light.
    // This additive approximation omits surface fog extinction and multiple scattering.
    let scattering = radiance * uniforms.volume_params.w;
    return source + scattering / (vec3(1.0) + scattering);
}
// Intermediate alpha remains material metadata; final output alpha is opaque. Keep SSR before color-only effects in EFFECTS by default.
@fragment fn fs_ssr(input: Output) -> @location(0) vec4<f32> {
    let source = scene_at(input.uv);
    var surface = source;
    let depth = depth_at(input.uv);
    if uniforms.camera.w > 0.5 && source.a > 0.001 && depth < 0.999999 {
        let wetness = reflection_mask_at(world_at(input.uv, depth));
        surface = vec4(source.rgb * mix(1.0, 0.58, wetness), source.a);
    }
    return vec4(reflected_color(input.uv, surface), select(source.a, 1.0, uniforms.params.y > 0.5));
}
@fragment fn fs_god_rays(input: Output) -> @location(0) vec4<f32> {
    let source = scene_at(input.uv);
    return vec4(god_rays(input.uv, source.rgb), select(source.a, 1.0, uniforms.params.y > 0.5));
}

\n# File: src/render/present.wgsl
@group(0) @binding(0) var scene_texture: texture_2d<f32>;
@group(0) @binding(1) var scene_sampler: sampler;
struct Output { @builtin(position) position: vec4<f32>, @location(0) uv: vec2<f32> };
@vertex fn vs_main(@builtin(vertex_index) index: u32) -> Output {
    let positions = array<vec2<f32>, 3>(vec2(-1.0, -1.0), vec2(3.0, -1.0), vec2(-1.0, 3.0));
    let p = positions[index];
    var output: Output;
    output.position = vec4(p, 0.0, 1.0);
    output.uv = vec2((p.x + 1.0) * 0.5, (1.0 - p.y) * 0.5);
    return output;
}
@fragment fn fs_main(input: Output) -> @location(0) vec4<f32> {
    return textureSample(scene_texture, scene_sampler, input.uv);
}
